### [1] [Slowly rotating traversable wormholes supported by radially varying string-fluid matter: From regular geometries to photon trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11261)
*A. Errehymy,B. Turimov,M. A. Khan,S. Usanov,Z. Yasakov,Z. Avezmuratova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper constructs slowly rotating traversable wormhole solutions supported by radially varying string fluids, which smoothly interpolate between a de Sitter-like core and an asymptotically flat spacetime. The anisotropic fluid’s transverse pressure depends on radius, ensuring regularity and horizon-free geometry. Rotation induces frame-dragging that subtly affects photon trajectories, with distinct co- and counter-rotating paths near the throat. Different radial profiles of the string fluid produce unique photon-ring signatures, offering observational handles on both rotation and internal matter structure.
\n\n
本文构建了由径向变化的弦流体支撑的慢速旋转可穿越虫洞解，该流体在类de Sitter核心与渐近平直时空之间平滑插值。各向异性流体的横向压强依赖于径向坐标，从而保证了几何的正则性且无视界。旋转引入参考系拖曳效应，轻微影响光子轨迹，在虫洞喉部附近产生明显的顺转与逆转路径差异。弦流体的不同径向分布会产生独特的光子环特征，为观测虫洞旋转及内部物质结构提供了可能的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to construct physically plausible, regular, and horizonless wormhole geometries that incorporate slow rotation and are sourced by realistic anisotropic matter—specifically, string fluids whose properties vary with radius. Standard wormhole models often rely on exotic matter violating energy conditions or assume staticity; this work aims to relax these limitations by allowing radial dependence in the fluid’s equation of state, thereby enabling smooth transitions between different physical regimes (e.g., vacuum-like core to string-dominated exterior) while maintaining asymptotic flatness and supporting rotational effects relevant for astrophysical observability.
\n\n
动机在于构建物理上合理、正则且无视界的虫洞几何，包含慢速旋转，并由现实的各向异性物质（特别是其性质随半径变化的弦流体）提供支撑。标准虫洞模型通常依赖违反能量条件的奇异物质或假设静态；本文通过允许流体状态方程具有径向依赖性，旨在放宽这些限制，从而在不同物理区域（如类真空核心到弦主导外部）之间实现平滑过渡，同时保持渐近平直性并支持与天体物理可观测性相关的旋转效应。

Method: The authors employ a modified Morris–Thorne wormhole metric extended to include slow rotation via a linear-in-$\\Omega$ frame-dragging term, where $\\Omega$ is the angular velocity of the wormhole. The matter source is modeled as a string fluid with anisotropic stress: energy density $\\rho(r)$, radial pressure $p_r = -\\rho(r)$ (characteristic of strings), and transverse pressure $p_t(r)$ allowed to vary with radius. Einstein’s equations are solved under the assumption of slow rotation (first-order in $\\Omega$), yielding coupled differential equations for the redshift function $\\Phi(r)$, shape function $b(r)$, and the dragging potential $\\omega(r)$. Regularity at the throat and asymptotic flatness impose boundary conditions. Photon orbits are analyzed using the effective potential for null geodesics in the equatorial plane, distinguishing co-rotating ($+$) and counter-rotating ($-$) trajectories.
\n\n
作者采用修正的Morris–Thorne虫洞度规，并通过线性于$\\Omega$的参考系拖曳项引入慢速旋转（其中$\\Omega$为虫洞角速度）。物质源被建模为具有各向异性应力的弦流体：能量密度为$\\rho(r)$，径向压强$p_r = -\\rho(r)$（弦的特征），而横向压强$p_t(r)$允许随半径变化。在慢速旋转假设下（即对$\\Omega$作一阶展开），求解爱因斯坦方程，得到关于红移函数$\\Phi(r)$、形状函数$b(r)$和拖曳势$\\omega(r)$的耦合微分方程组。虫洞喉部的正则性和渐近平直性施加了边界条件。通过赤道面上零测地线的有效势分析光子轨道，区分顺转（$+$）与逆转（$-$）轨迹。

Result: The model yields regular, traversable, asymptotically flat wormhole solutions with no horizons. The radial dependence of $p_t(r)$ ensures finite energy density and smooth shape functions. Frame-dragging from slow rotation modifies photon trajectories asymmetrically: co-rotating photons orbit closer to the throat than counter-rotating ones. Circular photon orbits exist only within specific parameter ranges, and their radii depend sensitively on the interplay between $\\Phi(r)$, $b(r)$, and $\\omega(r)$. Distinct choices for the radial profile of the string fluid (e.g., exponential vs. power-law decay of $\\rho(r)$) lead to measurably different photon-ring structures, suggesting that future high-resolution observations (e.g., with next-generation EHT) could constrain the internal matter composition and rotation rate.
\n\n
该模型得到了正则、可穿越、渐近平直且无视界的虫洞解。$p_t(r)$的径向依赖性保证了有限的能量密度和平滑的形状函数。慢速旋转引起的参考系拖曳不对称地改变了光子轨迹：顺转光子轨道比逆转光子更靠近虫洞喉部。圆形光子轨道仅在特定参数范围内存在，其半径对$\\Phi(r)$、$b(r)$和$\\omega(r)$之间的相互作用高度敏感。弦流体不同的径向分布（如$\\rho(r)$的指数衰减与幂律衰减）会导致可测量的光子环结构差异，表明未来的高分辨率观测（如下一代事件视界望远镜EHT）或可约束虫洞内部物质组成与旋转速率。

Conclusion: Radially varying string fluids provide a physically consistent and flexible framework for constructing slowly rotating, traversable wormholes that are regular, horizonless, and asymptotically flat. The anisotropy and radial dependence of the matter source naturally accommodate geometric constraints while enabling rich phenomenology in light propagation. The resulting frame-dragging and photon-ring signatures offer promising observational avenues to distinguish such exotic compact objects from black holes, highlighting the role of anisotropic matter in shaping both spacetime curvature and observable optical features.
\n\n
径向变化的弦流体为构建慢速旋转、可穿越、正则、无视界且渐近平直的虫洞提供了一个物理自洽且灵活的框架。物质源的各向异性与径向依赖性自然满足几何约束，同时在光传播中展现出丰富的现象学特征。由此产生的参考系拖曳效应和光子环特征为将此类奇异致密天体与黑洞区分开来提供了有前景的观测途径，凸显了各向异性物质在塑造时空曲率和可观测光学特征中的关键作用。

Abstract: This work investigates slowly rotating traversable wormholes supported by string fluids whose properties vary with distance from the throat. This radial variation allows the matter to transition smoothly from a de Sitter-like core near the center to a string-dominated environment further out, producing a regular, horizon-free, and asymptotically flat spacetime. By letting the transverse pressure depend on radius, the fluid naturally adapts to the surrounding geometry, resulting in a well-behaved energy density and shape function. Even modest rotation introduces frame-dragging effects that gently twist photon paths, creating subtle differences between co-rotating and counter-rotating trajectories. These effects are strongest near the throat, while at larger distances the spacetime is largely governed by the static gravitational potentials. Circular photon orbits reveal that the interplay of the redshift function, wormhole shape, and rotation shapes the photon-sphere structure. Different radial profiles of the string fluid generate distinctive photon-ring patterns, offering potential observational signatures of both the rotation and the internal matter distribution. Overall, radially varying string fluids provide a flexible and physically consistent source for traversable wormholes, bridging smoothly between vacuum-like and string-dominated regions while maintaining regularity and supporting slow rotation. This study highlights how anisotropic matter can influence both curvature and light propagation, providing a realistic framework for horizonless exotic spacetimes and suggesting new avenues to explore subtle observational effects around traversable wormholes.

</details>


### [2] [Quasinormal modes and tidal responses of black holes in generic anisotropic matter environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11380)
*Yu-Qian Zhao,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper constructs a perturbative framework to study black holes in generic anisotropic matter environments under spherical symmetry, generalizing the Einstein cluster with polytropic radial pressure. It finds that while geodesic structure and axial quasinormal modes are mainly affected by gravitational redshift, tidal Love numbers show strong sensitivity to anisotropy—yielding order-unity, vanishing, or even negative static magnetic Love numbers.
\n\n
本文构建了一个微扰框架，用于研究球对称下嵌入在一般各向异性物质环境中的黑洞，将Einstein cluster推广至包含多方径向压强的情形。研究发现，测地线结构和轴向准正规模谱主要受整体引力红移效应支配，而潮汐Love数对各向异性极为敏感——可产生量级为1、甚至为零或负的静态磁性Love数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of black holes in external matter were limited either to vanishing radial pressure or to perturbative matter distributions lacking full anisotropy. The authors aim to develop a more general perturbative formalism under spherical symmetry that accommodates arbitrary (anisotropic) stress-energy tensors, enabling systematic investigation of environmental effects on black hole observables like quasinormal modes and tidal deformability.
\n\n
以往关于黑洞处于外部物质环境中的研究要么局限于径向压强为零的情形，要么仅考虑缺乏完整各向异性的微扰物质分布。作者旨在构建一个更普适的球对称微扰形式体系，能够容纳任意（各向异性）应力-能量张量，从而系统研究环境效应对黑洞可观测量（如准正规模和潮汐可形变性）的影响。

Method: The authors employ linearized Einstein equations around a Schwarzschild background under spherical symmetry, allowing for an anisotropic fluid with polytropic equation of state $p_r = K \\rho^\\Gamma$, where $p_r$ is radial pressure. They solve the perturbed field equations analytically to construct a generalized Einstein cluster solution. The geodesic deviation, axial quasinormal-mode spectrum, and static tidal Love numbers (both electric and magnetic) are then computed from the metric perturbations using standard post-Newtonian and black hole perturbation theory techniques.
\n\n
作者在球对称条件下对Schwarzschild背景应用线性化的Einstein方程，引入具有多方状态方程 $p_r = K \\rho^\\Gamma$ 的各向异性流体（其中 $p_r$ 为径向压强）。他们解析求解微扰场方程，构造出一个广义的Einstein cluster解。随后利用标准后牛顿理论和黑洞微扰理论方法，从度规微扰出发计算测地线偏离、轴向准正规模谱以及静态潮汐Love数（包括电型和磁型）。

Result: The generalized Einstein cluster solution exhibits environmental corrections dominated by gravitational redshift for orbital dynamics and axial quasinormal modes. However, the radial pressure significantly amplifies these corrections. Crucially, the static magnetic-type tidal Love numbers can vanish or become negative for large anisotropy parameters, indicating strong deviations from vacuum black hole behavior. The full set of linearized field equations is derived and made available for applications such as ringdown modeling and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs).
\n\n
广义Einstein cluster解在轨道动力学和轴向准正规模中表现出以引力红移为主导的环境修正；然而，径向压强显著放大了这些修正。关键的是，当各向异性参数足够大时，静态磁型潮汐Love数可变为零甚至负值，表明与真空黑洞行为存在显著偏离。文中推导了完整的线性化场方程，可用于铃宕（ringdown）建模和极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）等应用。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that anisotropic matter environments induce nontrivial modifications to black hole structure beyond simple redshift effects, particularly in tidal response. The possibility of vanishing or negative static magnetic Love numbers challenges the universality of black hole no-hair properties in realistic astrophysical settings and provides new observational signatures for testing gravity in strong-field regimes.
\n\n
本文表明，各向异性物质环境对黑洞结构的修正不仅限于简单的红移效应，尤其体现在潮汐响应上。静态磁型Love数可能为零或负值，这一结果挑战了在真实天体物理环境中黑洞“无毛”性质的普适性，并为强场引力检验提供了新的观测特征。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for a black hole embedded in a generic, possibly anisotropic, matter environment under spherical symmetry. Our approach extends previous analyses restricted to vanishing radial pressure or to perturbative matter configurations. Within this framework, we derive an analytical generalization of the Einstein cluster that incorporates a polytropic radial pressure, and we investigate the properties of this solution. We show that both the geodesic structure and the axial quasinormal-mode spectrum remain predominantly governed by an overall gravitational redshift effect, while the radial pressure systematically enhances the environmental corrections. In contrast, the tidal Love numbers are substantially more sensitive, and can exhibit order-unity deviations, including vanishing and negative strictly static magnetic Love numbers for sufficiently large anisotropy. We present the full linearized equations, which can be applied to various extensions, including ringdown analysis and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals.

</details>


### [3] [Linearized stability of T-duality quantum-inspired thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11413)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper constructs a traversable thin-shell wormhole by joining two copies of a quantum-corrected, regular black hole spacetime derived from string T-duality. The resulting geometry replaces the classical singularity with a smooth core characterized by a fundamental length scale $l_0$. The authors demonstrate that for sufficiently large throat radii, the null and strong energy conditions can be satisfied—unlike in classical Schwarzschild-based wormholes. A linear stability analysis uncovers a regime of unconditional stability near $a \sim l_0$, where any convex surface mass profile ensures stability, a feature absent in classical models. This suggests quantum-gravity-inspired corrections can yield dynamically viable, singularity-free traversable wormholes.
\n\n
本文通过拼接两个源自弦论T-duality的量子修正正则黑洞时空，构建了一种可穿越的薄壳虫洞。所得几何结构以光滑核心取代了经典曲率奇点，并引入了一个基本长度尺度 $l_0$。作者证明，对于足够大的喉半径，null energy condition 和 strong energy condition 均可满足——这与基于Schwarzschild的经典虫洞不同。线性稳定性分析揭示出在 $a \sim l_0$ 附近存在一个无条件稳定区域：任意凸的表面质量函数均可保证稳定性，这是经典模型所不具备的特性。这表明受量子引力启发的修正可同时消除奇点并使可穿越虫洞在动力学上可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical traversable wormholes typically violate standard energy conditions and are dynamically unstable, requiring exotic matter. Moreover, they often contain curvature singularities. The authors aim to explore whether quantum-gravity-motivated modifications—specifically those arising from string T-duality—can simultaneously resolve singularities and improve the energy and stability properties of thin-shell wormholes, potentially yielding physically plausible exotic compact objects.
\n\n
经典的可穿越虫洞通常违反标准能量条件且动力学不稳定，需要奇异物质（exotic matter），并且常包含曲率奇点。作者旨在探究受量子引力启发的修正——特别是源自弦论T-duality的修正——是否能同时解决奇点问题并改善薄壳虫洞的能量条件与稳定性，从而可能产生物理上合理的奇异致密天体（exotic compact objects）。

Method: The authors construct a thin-shell wormhole via the cut-and-paste method, gluing two identical copies of a quantum-corrected, regular black hole metric obtained from string T-duality. This metric features a minimal length scale $l_0$ and is free of curvature singularities. They compute the surface stress-energy tensor on the throat using the Darmois–Israel junction formalism. Energy conditions (null and strong) are analyzed as functions of the throat radius $a$. Linearized radial perturbations are then studied to assess dynamical stability, leading to a stability criterion expressed in terms of the surface mass function and its derivatives.
\n\n
作者采用切-贴（cut-and-paste）方法，将两个相同的、源自弦论T-duality的量子修正正则黑洞度规拼接，构造薄壳虫洞。该度规包含一个最小长度尺度 $l_0$ 且无曲率奇点。他们利用Darmois–Israel连接形式（junction formalism）计算喉部的表面应力-能量张量，并将null energy condition与strong energy condition表示为喉半径 $a$ 的函数进行分析。随后研究径向微扰的线性化动力学，导出以表面质量函数及其导数表达的稳定性判据。

Result: For static configurations, the null and strong energy conditions are satisfied when the throat radius $a$ exceeds a critical value, unlike in Schwarzschild-based wormholes. The linear stability analysis yields three distinct regimes: (i) instability near the minimal allowed $a$, (ii) an intermediate window around $a \sim l_0$ where the geometric stability threshold becomes negative, implying \emph{unconditional stability} for any convex surface mass function, and (iii) large-$a$ behavior resembling Schwarzschild wormholes, requiring a stiff equation of state ($\\eta^2 \\geq 1$) for stability. The scale $l_0$ thus governs both regularity and a novel stability mechanism.
\n\n
对于静态构型，当喉半径 $a$ 超过某一临界值时，null energy condition 与 strong energy condition 均可满足，这与基于Schwarzschild的虫洞不同。线性稳定性分析揭示出三个不同区域：(i) 在最小允许 $a$ 附近不稳定；(ii) 在 $a \sim l_0$ 的中间区域，几何稳定性阈值变为负值，意味着对任意凸的表面质量函数都存在\emph{无条件稳定性}；(iii) 在大 $a$ 极限下，行为趋近于Schwarzschild虫洞，此时稳定性要求刚性物态方程（$\\eta^2 \\geq 1$）。因此，尺度 $l_0$ 不仅实现正则化，还主导了一种全新的稳定性机制。

Conclusion: Quantum corrections inspired by string T-duality can produce singularity-free, traversable thin-shell wormholes that satisfy standard energy conditions and exhibit a novel regime of unconditional dynamical stability near the fundamental length scale $l_0$. This demonstrates that quantum gravity effects may render exotic compact objects physically viable, offering new theoretical paradigms and potential observational signatures in gravitational-wave astronomy.
\n\n
受弦论T-duality启发的量子修正可产生无奇点、可穿越的薄壳虫洞，其不仅满足标准能量条件，还在基本长度尺度 $l_0$ 附近展现出一种全新的无条件动力学稳定区域。这表明量子引力效应可能使奇异致密天体在物理上可行，为理论研究和引力波天文学中的新观测目标提供了新范式。

Abstract: Wormholes that are traversable in principle offer fascinating insights into general relativity, yet they typically require exotic matter and suffer from stability issues. We construct a thin-shell wormhole by gluing two copies of a quantum-corrected, regular spacetime obtained from string T-duality. This regularisation replaces the classical curvature singularity with a smooth core and introduces a fundamental length scale $l_0$. For the static configuration, we derive the surface stresses and show that, unlike the Schwarzschild case, the null and strong energy conditions can be satisfied for sufficiently large throat radii. A linearised stability analysis reveals a rich landscape: close to the minimum allowed throat radius the configuration is unstable; at intermediate radii ($a \sim l_0$) the geometric stability threshold becomes negative, yielding a window of \emph{unconditional stability} where any convex surface mass function suffices; at large radii the wormhole recovers Schwarzschild-like behaviour and stability requires a stiff equation of state. The T-duality scale $l_0$ is thus not merely a regulariser but a key physical parameter that opens a novel region of unconditional stability absent in classical thin-shell wormholes. Our results suggest that quantum-gravity-motivated modifications can simultaneously cure singularities and make traversable wormholes dynamically viable, providing new targets for gravitational-wave astronomy and theoretical studies of exotic compact objects.

</details>


### [4] [On phase-space singular surfaces in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11453)
*Dražen Glavan,David M. J. Vokrouhlický*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper analyzes Hamiltonian constraints in metric $f(R)$ gravity in the Jordan frame, revealing that the standard constraint classification breaks down on singular phase-space surfaces defined by $f'(R)=0$ and $f''(R)=0$. It shows that backgrounds satisfying $f(R)=0$ and $f'(R)=0$ yield an empty linearized spectrum—a generalization of the pure $R^2$ case—and demonstrates that FLRW solutions in the Starobinsky model can cross $f'(R)=0$, but perturbations develop degenerate constraints at the crossing, interpreted as a regularity condition rather than a Dirac--Bergmann constraint.
\n\n
本文分析了Jordan框架下度规$f(R)$引力的哈密顿约束，揭示出标准约束分类在由$f'(R)=0$和$f''(R)=0$定义的奇异相空间表面上失效。研究表明，满足$f(R)=0$和$f'(R)=0$的背景导致线性化谱为空——这是纯$R^2$情形的推广；并证明Starobinsky模型中的FLRW轨迹可穿越$f'(R)=0$表面，但非均匀扰动在穿越点处发展出退化的约束结构，应被解释为扰动演化的正则性条件，而非Dirac--Bergmann算法中的普通约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the Hamiltonian structure and constraint algebra of metric $f(R)$ gravity beyond generic backgrounds, particularly near singular surfaces in phase space where $f'(R)=0$ or $f''(R)=0$. These surfaces signal potential breakdowns in the standard Dirac--Bergmann constraint analysis, which could affect the number of physical degrees of freedom and the consistency of perturbation theory—especially relevant for cosmological models like the Starobinsky $R+R^2$ inflation scenario.
\n\n
动机在于理解度规$f(R)$引力在相空间奇异表面（即$f'(R)=0$或$f''(R)=0$）附近的哈密顿结构与约束代数，这些表面可能标志标准Dirac--Bergmann约束分析的失效，从而影响物理自由度数目及扰动理论的一致性——这对Starobinsky $R+R^2$暴胀等宇宙学模型尤为重要。

Method: The authors apply the Dirac--Bergmann algorithm to metric $f(R)$ gravity in the Jordan frame, performing a full Hamiltonian constraint analysis. They identify primary and secondary constraints and examine their classification (first- vs second-class) on and off the singular surfaces $f'(R)=0$ and $f''(R)=0$. They then linearize the theory around exact backgrounds satisfying $f(R)=0$ and $f'(R)=0$, and separately study FLRW backgrounds in the Starobinsky model that dynamically cross $f'(R)=0$, analyzing the behavior of inhomogeneous perturbations at the crossing.
\n\n
作者在Jordan框架下对度规$f(R)$引力应用Dirac--Bergmann算法，进行完整的哈密顿约束分析。他们识别初级与次级约束，并检验其在奇异表面$f'(R)=0$和$f''(R)=0$上/下的分类（第一类vs第二类）。随后，他们在满足$f(R)=0$和$f'(R)=0$的精确背景上线性化理论，并单独研究Starobinsky模型中动态穿越$f'(R)=0$的FLRW背景，分析穿越点处非均匀扰动的行为。

Result: The constraint structure degenerates on the surfaces $f'(R)=0$ and $f''(R)=0$. Backgrounds with $f(R)=0$ and $f'(R)=0$ have no propagating linear degrees of freedom—the pure $R^2$ model is a special case. In the Starobinsky model, FLRW trajectories can cross $f'(R)=0$, but inhomogeneous perturbations exhibit a degenerate constraint algebra at the crossing point. This degeneracy manifests not as a standard constraint but as a regularity condition necessary for consistent perturbative evolution.
\n\n
约束结构在$f'(R)=0$和$f''(R)=0$表面上退化。满足$f(R)=0$和$f'(R)=0$的背景无线性传播自由度——纯$R^2$模型是其特例。在Starobinsky模型中，FLRW轨迹可穿越$f'(R)=0$，但非均匀扰动在穿越点处表现出退化的约束代数。这种退化并非表现为标准约束，而是作为保证扰动演化一致性的正则性条件出现。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that singular phase-space surfaces in $f(R)$ gravity lead to distinct types of perturbative degeneracies depending on whether the background lies entirely on the surface or crosses it dynamically. This distinction refines the understanding of degrees of freedom and constraint propagation in modified gravity, showing that crossings of $f'(R)=0$ require imposing regularity conditions on perturbations rather than standard constraints.
\n\n

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate energy extraction via the Comisso-Asenjo (CA) magnetic reconnection process near a coupled $\mathcal{N}=2,\,U(1)^2$ gauged supergravity Black Hole (BH). Our study focuses on the combined impact of the independent parameter set $p_i\in(N_g,g,v,e)$ with the spin parameter $a$ on the extracted energy ($ε_{\pm}$), efficiency ($η$), and extracted power ($\mathcal{P}_{CA}$), aiming to identify optimal combinations where energy can be extracted with higher efficiency in certain cases at lower spin $(a\sim0.39)$ than the Kerr extremal case $(a\sim1)$. Using the spacetime parameters, we explore various cases leading to distinct spacetimes and provide an extended comparison with the Kerr Black Hole (KBH). We also examine the influence of the orientation angle ($ξ$) and magnetization parameter ($σ_0$) on both efficiency and extracted power. Investigating low $[\,\forall p_i<0.2 \land N_g<0.08\,]$, mid $[\,\exists p_i\ge0.5 \land N_g\in(0.08,0.15)\,]$, high $[\,\exists p_i>0.7 \land N_g\in(0.16,0.23)\,]$, and mixed $[\,\forall p_i\in(0,1) \land N_g\in(0,0.23)\,]$ parameter combinations, we explore only extremal cases for all spacetime parameters and demonstrate that the extremal Kerr efficiency limit ($η>1.495$) can be exceeded. The statistical Kendall's Tau approach allows us to identify the key independent parameters acting as boosters or dampers in the energy extraction process and to visualize the relationship between $(N_g,g,v,e)$ and the physical outputs $(a_{\rm ext},r_E,r_{\rm ergo},ε_{\pm},η,\mathcal{P}_{CA},R_η,R_{\mathcal{P}})$. Furthermore, we show that the observable Lundquist number $S_{\rm obs}$ in rotating BH spacetimes acquires an observer-dependent angular dependence through the lapse function $(α)$. This leads to deviations from the standard Sweet-Parker scaling when expressed in terms of observable quantities.

Result: The fundamental field equations of $κ(R,T)$ gravity are shown to be regular at $κ=0$. A necessary compatibility condition $(\\nabla^μκ)T_{μν}=0$ must hold on critical hypersurfaces. 研究表明，$κ(R,T)$引力的基本场方程在$κ=0$处是正则的。临界超曲面上必须满足相容性条件$(\\nabla^μκ)T_{μν}=0$。这些曲面充当引力屏蔽边界，分隔引力吸引（$κ>0$）与排斥（$κ<0$）区域。此外，它们的存在阻碍了向全局Einstein-frame的共形变换，这与某些其他修改引力理论不同。

Conclusion: Critical hypersurfaces with $κ=0$ are physically meaningful and mathematically consistent features of $κ(R,T)$ gravity. Abstract: We investigate the critical regime $κ(R,T)=0$ in $κ(R,T)$ gravity. While most studies assume a non-vanishing effective gravitational coupling, the existence of critical hypersurfaces where $κ$ vanishes is a generic feature of many admissible coupling functions. We show that the apparent singularity of the non-conservation equation is an artifact of a rewritten form of the conservation law and that the fundamental equations remain regular at $κ=0$. We further analyze the structure of critical hypersurfaces, derive the associated compatibility condition $(\nabla^μκ)T_{μν}=0$, and discuss their interpretation as gravitational screening surfaces separating attractive and repulsive gravitational phases. The existence of critical coupling hypersurfaces also obstructs a global Einstein-frame description, distinguishing $κ(R,T)$ gravity from theories based solely on algebraic redefinitions of the energy-momentum tensor. Possible cosmological and astrophysical consequences are briefly explored.

Method: The authors construct a minimal predictive cosmological model based on quantum post-selection and coarse-graining, extending prior work on post-selected quasiclassical dynamics. They derive an effective Friedmann-like expansion history that incorporates late-time acceleration without $\\Lambda$, dark energy, or modifications to general relativity. The model introduces at most two free parameters beyond standard cosmology and computes observables such as the Hubble parameter $H(z)$ and jerk parameter $j_0$. They confront the model with type Ia supernova and cosmic chronometer data using standard statistical methods.

作者基于量子后选择与粗粒化构建了一个极简且具有预测能力的宇宙学模型，拓展了此前关于后选择准经典动力学的工作。他们推导出一个类Friedmann的有效膨胀历史，在不引入$\\Lambda$、暗能量或修改广义相对论的前提下实现晚期加速。该模型在标准宇宙学之外最多引入两个自由参数，并计算哈勃参数$H(z)$和急动参数$j_0$等可观测量。作者使用标准统计方法将模型与Ia型超新星和宇宙计时器（cosmic chronometer）数据进行对比。

Conclusion: Cosmic acceleration may not require new substances or modified gravity, but could instead emerge as a macroscopic quantum effect from post-selection—a fundamental yet previously overlooked aspect of quantum theory in cosmology. Abstract: The origin of cosmic acceleration remains a central problem in cosmology, commonly attributed to a cosmological constant within the $Λ$CDM model or to dynamical dark energy. Here, we develop an alternative approach in which acceleration emerges from quantum post-selection, a standard feature of quantum theory that is not usually incorporated into cosmological modelling. While quantum theory admits both pre-selected and post-selected ensembles, quantum cosmological models are almost exclusively formulated in terms of initial conditions. Building on previous work on post-selected quasiclassical dynamics, we construct a minimal predictive cosmological model in which post-selection and coarse-graining generate effective late-time acceleration without introducing a cosmological constant, dark energy, or modifications of general relativity. The resulting expansion history is highly constrained theoretically and depends on at most two parameters beyond standard Friedmann evolution. Confrontation with type Ia supernova and cosmic chronometer data yields statistically competitive fits while naturally avoiding the coincidence problem. The model also reproduces the standard radiation- and matter-dominated behaviour at early times and predicts a present-day jerk parameter significantly different from the $Λ$CDM value. These results suggest that cosmic acceleration may arise as a macroscopic quantum cosmological effect rather than from additional cosmological fluids or modified gravitational dynamics.

### [11] [Bouncing Geodesics, Singularities, and the Cavity Thermal Product Formula in Asymptotically Flat and de Sitter Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11297)
*Sašo Grozdanov,Vita Movrin,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies ``bouncing geodesics''—null or timelike trajectories that reflect near the black hole singularity—in asymptotically flat and Schwarzschild–de Sitter spacetimes. These geodesics induce singularities in the bulk retarded Green's function, whose timing is precisely determined using Hadamard parametrix and global propagation of singularities. For black holes in a reflecting cavity, the authors derive a thermal product formula linking the locations of these bouncing singularities to the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum, enabling interior reconstruction from boundary QNM data—even for $\\Lambda \\geq 0$. This is verified via explicit computations for scalar, electromagnetic, and gravitational perturbations.
\n\n
本文研究了在渐近平直和Schwarzschild–de Sitter时空中“反弹测地线”（bouncing geodesics）——即在黑洞奇点附近发生反射的类光或类时轨迹。这些测地线会在体空间推迟Green函数中引发奇点，其出现时间通过Hadamard形式与奇点传播定理被精确确定。对于置于反射腔中的黑洞，作者推导出一个热积公式（thermal product formula），将反弹奇点的位置与准正规模（quasinormal mode, QNM）谱联系起来，从而即使在宇宙学常数$\\Lambda \\geq 0$的情况下，也能从边界QNM数据重构黑洞内部信息。该预言通过标量场、电磁场及引力波的腔QNM显式计算得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the desire to probe the black hole interior—particularly the high-curvature region near the singularity—using boundary observables. In anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes, the AdS/CFT correspondence allows such interior reconstruction via the thermal product formula relating Green's function singularities to quasinormal modes. However, for asymptotically flat or de Sitter black holes ($\\Lambda \\geq 0$), no natural timelike boundary exists, making such reconstruction nontrivial. The authors address this by introducing an artificial reflecting cavity, thereby creating a well-defined boundary where QNMs can be measured, and ask whether a similar connection between interior geodesic singularities and QNM spectra persists outside AdS.
\n\n
动机源于利用边界可观测量探测黑洞内部（尤其是奇点附近的高曲率区域）的愿望。在反de Sitter（AdS）时空中，AdS/CFT对偶允许通过热积公式将Green函数奇点与准正规模联系起来，从而实现内部重构。然而，对于渐近平直或de Sitter黑洞（$\\Lambda \\geq 0$），不存在自然的类时边界，使得此类重构变得困难。作者通过引入人工反射腔来解决这一问题，从而构造一个可明确定义的边界以测量QNM，并探究在非AdS情形下，内部测地线奇点与QNM谱之间是否存在类似的关联。

Method: The authors combine microlocal analysis with geometric optics: they use the Hadamard form of the Green's function to characterize short-distance singularities and apply the global propagation of singularities theorem to track how these singularities propagate along null geodesics into the bulk. They identify ``bouncing geodesics'' that originate from a boundary point, fall toward the singularity, and reflect due to the effective potential barrier near $r=0$, returning to the boundary. The critical times of Green's function singularities are computed for all anchorings (e.g., future null infinity $\\mathscr{I}^+$ or cosmological horizon). For cavities, they impose Dirichlet (or similar) boundary conditions at finite radius and compute the discrete QNM spectrum. They then derive a cavity analog of the thermal product formula: $$ G_{\\text{ret}}(t) \\sim \\sum_n \\frac{e^{-i \\omega_n t}}{\\omega - \\omega_n}, $$ and show that its singular support matches the arrival times of bouncing geodesics.
\n\n
作者结合微局部分析与几何光学方法：利用Green函数的Hadamard形式刻画短程奇点，并应用奇点全局传播定理追踪这些奇点如何沿类光测地线传播至体空间。他们识别出“反弹测地线”——从边界点出发、落向奇点、并在$r=0$附近的有效势垒处反射后返回边界的轨迹。针对所有锚定点（如未来类光无穷远$\\mathscr{I}^+$或宇宙学视界），计算Green函数奇点的临界时间。对于反射腔，他们在有限半径处施加Dirichlet（或类似）边界条件并计算离散QNM谱。随后推导出腔版本的热积公式：$$ G_{\\text{ret}}(t) \\sim \\sum_n \\frac{e^{-i \\omega_n t}}{\\omega - \\omega_n}, $$ 并证明其奇异支集与反弹测地线的到达时间一致。

Result: The paper establishes that bouncing geodesics produce sharp singularities in the retarded Green's function at calculable times determined by the geodesic length. For black holes in a reflecting cavity, the authors derive a precise correspondence: the singular support of $G_{\\text{ret}}$ coincides with the set of times $t = \\pm T_{\\text{bounce}} + 2\\pi n / \\kappa_{\\text{eff}}$, where $T_{\\text{bounce}}$ is the round-trip time of the bouncing geodesic and $\\kappa_{\\text{eff}}$ is an effective surface gravity governing the QNM spacing. Explicit numerical computations for scalar, electromagnetic, and gravitational perturbations in Schwarzschild and Schwarzschild–de Sitter cavities confirm that the asymptotic QNM spectrum encodes $T_{\\text{bounce}}$, thus validating the cavity thermal product formula for $\\Lambda \\geq 0$.
\n\n
本文确立了反弹测地线会在推迟Green函数中产生尖锐奇点，其出现时间由测地线长度精确决定。对于置于反射腔中的黑洞，作者建立了明确对应关系：$G_{\\text{ret}}$的奇异支集与时间集合$t = \\pm T_{\\text{bounce}} + 2\\pi n / \\kappa_{\\text{eff}}$重合，其中$T_{\\text{bounce}}$为反弹测地线的往返时间，$\\kappa_{\\text{eff}}$为控制QNM间距的有效表面引力。通过对Schwarzschild和Schwarzschild–de Sitter腔中标量场、电磁场及引力扰动的显式数值计算，证实了渐近QNM谱确实编码了$T_{\\text{bounce}}$，从而验证了在$\\Lambda \\geq 0$情形下腔热积公式的正确性。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that information about the black hole interior—specifically the geometry near the singularity—can be extracted from boundary measurements of quasinormal modes, even in spacetimes with zero or positive cosmological constant, provided a reflecting boundary is introduced. This extends the interior reconstruction paradigm beyond AdS and establishes a universal link between geodesic singularities in Green's functions and the asymptotic QNM spectrum via a thermal product formula. The result reinforces the idea that QNMs are not merely boundary resonances but carry imprints of deep interior physics.
\n\n
本工作表明，只要引入反射边界，即使在宇宙学常数为零或正的时空中，也能通过边界准正规模测量提取黑洞内部（特别是奇点附近几何）的信息。这将内部重构范式推广到了AdS之外，并通过热积公式在Green函数中的测地线奇点与渐近QNM谱之间建立了普适联系。该结果强化了如下观点：QNM不仅是边界共振，更携带了深层内部物理的印记。

Abstract: We investigate the existence and implications of ``bouncing geodesics'' in asymptotically flat Schwarzschild and Schwarzschild--de Sitter black holes. These trajectories, which probe the high-curvature regions near the black hole singularity, correspond to specific ``bouncing singularities'' in the bulk retarded Green's function. We provide a precise description of these singularities by combining the local Hadamard form with the global propagation of singularities theorem. We then derive the critical times at which the bulk retarded correlator becomes singular, considering all possible anchorings of the bouncing geodesics, including null infinity and the cosmological horizon. Finally, for black holes enclosed in a reflecting cavity, we establish a universal connection between the locations of the bouncing singularities and the spectrum of cavity quasinormal modes (QNMs) by deriving a cavity version of the thermal product formula, analogous to the one known for anti-de Sitter black holes. This relation allows one to extract information about the black hole interior from the asymptotic QNM spectrum measured at a reflecting hypersurface, even when the cosmological constant is zero or positive. We confirm this prediction through explicit examples by computing the cavity QNMs of scalar and electromagnetic fields, as well as gravitational waves, in spacetimes with asymptotically flat and de Sitter black holes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to fill a gap in the CFT literature by offering a coherent, pedagogical introduction to composite operators—especially $\\phi^n$—through a semiclassical method valid at large operator charge $n$. This approach leverages the physical intuition that such heavy operators create highly occupied states, whose energy on the cylinder $\\mathbb{R} \\times S^{d-1}$ equals the scaling dimension via the state-operator correspondence.
\n\n
动机在于填补共形场论文献中的一个空白：通过适用于大算符荷 $n$ 的半经典方法，为复合算符（特别是 $\\phi^n$）提供连贯且教学性强的介绍。该方法基于物理图像：这类重算符会生成高度占据的态，根据态-算符对应关系，其在柱面 $\\mathbb{R} \\times S^{d-1}$ 上的能量即为标度维数。

Method: The method combines radial quantization, the state-operator correspondence, and semiclassical quantization techniques. It begins with free scalar theory to compute $\\Delta_{\\phi^n}$ in three ways, then generalizes via a double-scaling limit ($n \\to \\infty$, coupling $\\to 0$ with $n \\cdot g$ fixed). Key tools include action-angle variables, Bohr-Sommerfeld quantization, periodic saddle-point solutions, Floquet theory for time-periodic systems, fluctuation determinants computed via the Gel'fand-Yaglom method, and the Gutzwiller trace formula for spectral density.

方法结合了径向量子化、态-算符对应关系和半经典量子化技术。首先在自由标量理论中以三种方式计算 $\\Delta_{\\phi^n}$，然后通过双重缩放极限（$n \\to \\infty$，耦合常数 $\\to 0$，但保持 $n \\cdot g$ 固定）进行推广。关键工具包括作用量-角变量、Bohr-Sommerfeld 量子化、周期性鞍点解、针对时间周期系统的 Floquet 理论、通过 Gel'fand-Yaglom 方法计算的涨落行列式，以及用于谱密度的 Gutzwiller 迹公式。

对于 $d = 4 - \\varepsilon$ 维下 Wilson-Fisher 不动点处的 $O(N)$ $\\phi^4$ 理论，讲义推导出：(i) 柱面上的经典椭圆（椭圆余弦型）解；(ii) 围绕该背景的 Lamé 型涨落谱；(iii) 零模的识别与处理；以及 (iv) 大 $n$ 标度维数 $\\Delta_n$ 的完整一阶圈修正。结果验证了预期的展开形式 $\\Delta_n \\sim n^{\\frac{d}{d-2}}$，并给出了次领头的量子修正。

Conclusion: Composite operators with large charge serve as powerful probes of non-perturbative and collective dynamics in quantum field theory. The semiclassical framework developed here—rooted in CFT, integrable structures, and quantum chaos tools—offers a systematic and computationally tractable route to scaling dimensions beyond perturbation theory, with potential applications to gauge theories, conformal windows, and asymptotically safe scenarios.
\n\n
大荷复合算符可作为探测量子场论中非微扰和集体动力学的有力探针。本文发展的半经典框架——植根于共形场论、可积结构和量子混沌工具——提供了一条系统且计算可行的路径，用于超越微扰论计算标度维数，并可推广至规范理论、共形窗和渐近安全情形。

Abstract: These lecture notes are intended as a coherent introduction to conformal field theory in general, and composite operators in particular, through a semiclassical framework for computing scaling dimensions, with emphasis on operators of the form $φ^n$. In doing so, they aim to fill a gap in the literature and to help decode some of the relevant concepts. The physical idea is that at large $n$ an (heavy) operator creates a highly occupied state. Through the state-operator correspondence, this state lives on the cylinder $\mathbb{R}\times S^{d-1}$, and its scaling dimension is the corresponding energy of the theory on the cylinder. The notes are organized as a self-contained route from conformal symmetry to semiclassical dynamics. Part I reviews the conformal group, primary operators, radial quantization, the state-operator correspondence, and operator mixing. Part II builds the semiclassical framework, first in the free scalar theory, where the dimension of $φ^n$ is recovered in three independent ways, and then through the double-scaling limit, the action variable, and Bohr-Sommerfeld quantization. Part III develops the general machinery of periodic saddles, Floquet theory, fluctuation determinants, the Gel'fand-Yaglom method, and the Gutzwiller trace formula. Part IV applies the framework to the $O(N)$ $φ^4$ theory in $d=4-ε$ at the Wilson-Fisher fixed point, deriving the classical elliptic solution, the Lamé fluctuation spectrum, the zero modes, and the one-loop contribution to the large-$n$ scaling dimensions. Beyond the explicit computation, the notes emphasize the role of composite operators as probes of collective sectors of quantum field theory, with extensions to gauge theories, conformal windows, and asymptotically safe field theories.

### [13] [Weak-field waveforms for generic relativistic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11423)
*Stefano De Angelis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper reformulates Einstein's equations as integro-differential equations for particle worldlines using the Schwinger-Keldysh path integral, enabling independent computation of equations of motion and gravitational waveforms without requiring a scattering-to-bound-orbit map or integration-by-parts identities. This framework naturally incorporates retardation and radiation effects and offers an alternative or complement to Effective One-Body methods.
\n\n
该论文利用Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分将Einstein方程重构为关于粒子worldline的积分-微分方程，使得运动方程和引力波形的计算相互独立，无需散射与束缚轨道观测量之间的映射或分部积分恒等式。该框架自然包含推迟效应和辐射效应，并为Effective One-Body方法提供了替代或补充方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional post-Newtonian and Effective One-Body (EOB) approaches to binary dynamics often require separating potential and radiation modes and rely on integration-by-parts (IBP) identities, which become computationally prohibitive at high orders. Moreover, connecting scattering amplitudes to bound orbits introduces additional complexity. The authors seek a unified framework that avoids these bottlenecks while automatically incorporating radiation-reaction and retardation effects directly from first principles.
\n\n
传统的后牛顿（post-Newtonian）和Effective One-Body（EOB）方法在处理双星动力学时常需分离势模和辐射模，并依赖分部积分（integration-by-parts, IBP）恒等式，这在高阶计算中变得极其困难。此外，将散射振幅与束缚轨道关联也引入了额外复杂性。作者旨在构建一个统一框架，避免这些技术瓶颈，同时从第一性原理出发自动包含辐射反作用（radiation-reaction）和推迟效应。

Method: The authors employ the Schwinger-Keldysh (in-in) path integral formalism to integrate out the gravitational field, yielding causal integro-differential equations of motion for the worldlines $z^\\mu_i(\\tau)$. The same effective action governs both the dynamics and the emitted gravitational waveform $h_{\\mu\\nu}(x)$. The method operates in the weak-field expansion, with computations organized at leading order (LO) and next-to-leading order (NLO). Crucially, it avoids IBP reductions by working directly with time-domain Green’s functions and retarded propagators, and does not assume any amplitude-to-orbit dictionary.
\n\n
作者采用Schwinger-Keldysh（in-in）路径积分形式，对引力场进行泛函积分，从而导出关于worldline $z^\\mu_i(\\tau)$ 的因果型积分-微分运动方程。同一有效作用量同时支配动力学演化和辐射的引力波形 $h_{\\mu\\nu}(x)$。该方法在弱场展开下进行，计算按领头阶（LO）和次领头阶（NLO）组织。关键在于，通过直接使用时域Green函数和推迟传播子，避免了IBP约化，且不依赖任何振幅到轨道的对应字典。

Result: The paper establishes a self-contained framework where: (1) equations of motion are derived as retarded integro-differential equations encoding radiation reaction; (2) gravitational waveforms are computed independently via the same effective action; (3) no separation between near-zone (potential) and far-zone (radiation) degrees of freedom is needed; and (4) IBP identities—central to traditional amplitude-based methods—are entirely bypassed. Explicit strategies for LO and NLO computations are outlined, demonstrating feasibility.
\n\n
论文建立了一个自洽的框架：（1）运动方程被导出为包含辐射反作用的推迟型积分-微分方程；（2）引力波形通过同一有效作用量独立计算；（3）无需区分近区（势）与远区（辐射）自由度；（4）完全绕过了传统基于振幅方法所依赖的核心技术——IBP恒等式。文中还给出了LO和NLO计算的具体策略，证明了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: This Schwinger-Keldysh-based worldline framework provides a novel, first-principles approach to relativistic two-body dynamics and gravitational-wave emission that unifies dynamics and radiation without ad hoc splits or reliance on scattering amplitudes. It offers a promising alternative to current EFT and EOB paradigms, particularly for high-precision waveform modeling where IBP reductions become intractable.
\n\n
这一基于Schwinger-Keldysh的worldline框架为相对论性二体动力学和引力波辐射提供了一种新颖的第一性原理方法，无需人为分割或依赖散射振幅即可统一描述动力学与辐射。它为当前的EFT和EOB范式提供了有前景的替代方案，尤其适用于IBP约化变得不可行的高精度波形建模。

Abstract: We recast Einstein's equations as ordinary integro-differential equations for the worldlines, integrating out the gravitational field by means of the Schwinger-Keldysh path integral. The same framework allows the gravitational waveform to be computed for unspecified orbits. The two computations are independent: solutions of the equations of motion can then be inserted to reconstruct the waveform for generic orbits. The derivation of the equations of motion does not require a map between scattering and bound-orbit observables. Thus, it could be implemented within an Effective One-Body-inspired framework, with the advantage that retardation and radiation effects are automatically included: no separation between potential and radiation modes is required. Conversely, the waveform computation may provide an alternative to the Effective One-Body approach, if supplemented by suitable resummation schemes. We emphasise that computations in this framework bypass the need for integration-by-parts identities, which are the main technical bottleneck in the computation of observables. In this paper, we outline the general framework and present a computational strategy at leading and next-to-leading order in the weak-field expansion.

</details>

*Thiago Oliveira Ferreira,Andrei Mikhailov*

本文利用 BRST 算子与李代数上同调构成的双复形及其谱序列，研究了 $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ 的无穷小形变在 AdS 超对称代数下的表示结构，特别澄清了 ghost number 为三的零模结构。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The representation-theoretic structure of infinitesimal deformations of the $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ background—central to type IIB superstring theory—is not fully understood. $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ 背景（IIB 型超弦理论的核心）的无穷小形变的表示论结构尚未完全阐明。由于这些形变对应于一个幂零 BRST 算子的上同调，可借助同调代数工具来约束其如何组织成 AdS 超代数的表示。

Method: The authors construct a bicomplex combining the BRST differential $Q_{\text{BRST}}$ and the Lie algebra cohomology differential $d_{\text{Lie}}$, and analyze its two associated spectral sequences. By requiring consistency between the two spectral sequences (i.e., they must converge to the same total cohomology), they derive constraints on the possible representation content, particularly for states with ghost number three.
\n\n
作者构建了一个由 BRST 微分 $Q_{\\text{BRST}}$ 与李代数上同调微分 $d_{\\text{Lie}}$ 组成的双复形，并分析其两个相关的谱序列。通过要求两个谱序列的一致性（即必须收敛到相同的全上同调），推导出对可能表示内容的约束，尤其针对 ghost number 为三的态。

Result: The paper provides a clearer understanding of the ghost number three zero modes in the cohomology of the BRST operator. The matching conditions from the two spectral sequences restrict how these modes can appear in representations of the AdS supersymmetry algebra, offering partial classification of the deformation space.
\n\n
本文更清晰地理解了 BRST 算子上同调中 ghost number 为三的零模。来自两个谱序列的匹配条件限制了这些模在 AdS 超对称代数表示中的出现方式，从而对形变空间给出了部分分类。

Conclusion: The interplay between BRST cohomology and Lie algebra cohomology, encoded in the bicomplex and its spectral sequences, yields nontrivial constraints on the representation structure of $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ deformations. This approach opens a pathway toward a complete characterization of the supersymmetry representation content of the physical deformation space.
\n\n
BRST 上同调与李代数上同调之间的相互作用——通过双复形及其谱序列编码——对 $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ 形变的表示结构施加了非平凡约束。该方法为完整刻画物理形变空间的超对称表示内容开辟了路径。

Abstract: Infinitesimal deformations of $\text{AdS}_5 \times \mathbb{S}^5$ form a representation of the AdS supersymmetry algebra. The structure of this representation has not yet been completely described in the literature. Some information can be obtained just from the fact that the space of deformations is the cohomology of a nilpotent BRST operator. We can consider the bicomplex formed by the BRST operator and the Lie cohomology differential, and its two spectral sequences. Their matching imposes some constraints on the structure of representations, which we start exploring in this paper. In particular, we clarify the structure of ghost number three zero modes.

</details>


### [15] [Non-self-dual nontopological soliton in a pure Chern-Simons gauge model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11612)
*A. Yu. Loginov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates nontopological Q-ball solitons in a Chern-Simons-Higgs gauge theory beyond the self-dual limit, showing that such solitons exist as energy extrema at fixed Noether charge and that their existence and scaling properties critically depend on the scalar field's self-interaction potential—specifically requiring two degenerate zero minima for arbitrarily large energy and charge.
\n\n
本文研究了Chern-Simons-Higgs规范理论中非自对偶情形下的非拓扑Q-ball孤子，表明这类孤子在固定Noether荷下是能量泛函的极值，并且其存在性与标度性质强烈依赖于标量场的自相互作用势——特别是只有当该势具有两个简并零极小值时，孤子的能量和电荷才能取任意大的值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the structure and existence conditions of nontopological solitons (Q-balls) in (2+1)-dimensional Chern-Simons-Higgs models without assuming the self-dual limit, which restricts dynamics. The authors aim to uncover how the scalar self-interaction potential governs soliton properties like energy, charge, and stability, especially in the general non-supersymmetric case.
\n\n
动机在于理解(2+1)维Chern-Simons-Higgs模型中非拓扑孤子（Q-balls）的结构与存在条件，而不假设自对偶极限（该极限会限制动力学）。作者旨在揭示标量场自相互作用势如何决定孤子的能量、电荷和稳定性等性质，尤其是在一般的非超对称情形下。

Method: The authors employ variational methods to show that the soliton solution extremizes the energy functional under a fixed Noether charge constraint. They derive a differential relation among energy $E$, Noether charge $Q$, and the boundary value of the gauge potential $A_0(\infty)$ using scaling arguments and the equations of motion. Numerical integration of the coupled nonlinear field equations is used to map out the parametric domain of soliton existence and to analyze dependence on the scalar potential form.
\n\n
作者采用变分方法证明孤子解在固定Noether荷约束下使能量泛函取极值。他们利用标度论证和运动方程导出能量$E$、Noether荷$Q$与规范势边界值$A_0(\\infty)$之间的微分关系。通过数值求解耦合的非线性场方程，确定孤子存在的参数区域，并分析其对标量势形式的依赖性。

Result: Key results include: (1) the soliton is an extremum of energy at fixed $Q$; (2) a differential identity $dE/dQ = A_0(\infty)$ is derived; (3) a linear relation among kinetic, potential, and Chern-Simons contributions to total energy is found; (4) the soliton exists only within a restricted parameter region; and (5) arbitrarily large $E$ and $Q$ are possible only if the scalar self-interaction potential has two degenerate vacua at zero energy.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 孤子在固定$Q$下是能量的极值；(2) 导出了微分恒等式$dE/dQ = A_0(\\infty)$；(3) 发现总能量中动能、势能和Chern-Simons项之间存在线性关系；(4) 孤子仅存在于受限的参数区域内；(5) 只有当标量自相互作用势具有两个能量为零的简并真空时，$E$和$Q$才能取任意大的值。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that the existence and scaling behavior of Q-ball solitons in Chern-Simons-Higgs theory are highly sensitive to the shape of the scalar self-interaction potential. The requirement of degenerate minima for unbounded charge and energy highlights a deep connection between vacuum structure and soliton physics in non-self-dual gauge theories.
\n\n
研究表明，Chern-Simons-Higgs理论中Q-ball孤子的存在性与标度行为对标量自相互作用势的形状高度敏感。无界电荷与能量要求势具有简并极小值，这揭示了非自对偶规范理论中真空结构与孤子物理之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: A nontopological soliton of the Q-ball type in a Chern-Simons-Higgs gauge model is studied using both analytical and numerical methods. The general non-self-dual case is considered. It is shown that the soliton solution is an extremum of the energy functional at a fixed Noether charge. A differential relation between the energy, Noether charge, and the boundary value of the gauge potential of the soliton is derived. A linear relation between the components of the soliton energy is obtained. The parametric domain of existence of the soliton solution is determined. It is established that the soliton properties depend significantly on the form of the self-interaction potential of the scalar field. In particular, the energy and charge of the soliton can take arbitrarily large values only if the self-interaction potential has two degenerate zero minima.

</details>


### [16] [Modular quantization and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11984)
*Suchetan Das*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Witten's background-independent quantum gravity framework motivates a modular quantization of deformed CFT Hamiltonians on a cylinder, yielding type-I and type-III$_1$ von Neumann algebras depending on boundary conditions. This approach reproduces the exact Hartle-Hawking correlator of BTZ black holes in the semiclassical limit and reveals intrinsic non-smoothness (stretched horizon, boundary cutoff) at finite $G_N$, with microstructure emerging via an emergent center linked to AdS/CFT.
\n\n
Witten 提出的背景无关量子引力框架启发了对圆柱面上一类形变 CFT 哈密顿量的模（modular）量子化，根据边界条件可得到 I 型或 III$_1$ 型冯·诺依曼代数。该方法在半经典极限下重现了 BTZ 黑洞的精确 Hartle-Hawking 关联函数，并揭示了在有限 $G_N$ 下固有的非光滑性（拉伸视界、边界截断），其微观结构通过与 AdS/CFT 相关的“涌现中心”而显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper is motivated by Witten’s recent proposal of a background-independent algebraic formulation of quantum gravity, where an observer’s Hamiltonian defines a diffeomorphism-invariant worldline algebra via a modified Hamiltonian constraint. In the semiclassical regime, this lifts to a von Neumann algebra acting on a Hilbert space constructed from geodesics in a fixed background. The authors aim to explore non-perturbative black hole physics by applying similar algebraic and modular ideas to quantize deformed CFT Hamiltonians on a cylinder.
\n\n
本文受 Witten 最近提出的背景无关量子引力代数框架所启发：其中观测者的哈密顿量通过修正的哈密顿约束定义了一个微分同胚不变的世界线代数；在半经典极限下，该构造可提升为作用于由固定背景中测地线定义的希尔伯特空间上的冯·诺依曼代数。作者旨在将类似的代数与模结构思想应用于圆柱面上形变 CFT 哈密顿量的量子化，以探索黑洞的非微扰物理。

Method: The authors quantize a class of deformed CFT Hamiltonians on a cylinder using modular methods. They impose conformal boundary conditions near fixed points of the Hamiltonian flow to construct a type-I von Neumann algebra acting on a GNS Hilbert space built from highest-weight representations of an `emergent modular Virasoro algebra'. By identifying the Hamiltonian with the modular Hamiltonian of a sharp subregion relative to a reference KMS (vacuum) state, the algebra becomes a type-III$_1$ factor. They incorporate an emergent non-trivial center—composed of scalar fields localized at fixed points—via `open-closed string' duality to analyze the structure of the emergent Hilbert space. Finally, they compute boundary correlators in this modular quantization framework and compare them to the exact Hartle-Hawking correlator of the BTZ black hole.
\n\n
作者采用模方法对圆柱面上一类形变 CFT 哈密顿量进行量子化。他们在哈密顿流的不动点附近施加共形边界条件，构建了一个作用于 GNS 希尔伯特空间上的 I 型冯·诺依曼代数，该空间由“涌现模 Virasoro 代数”的最高权表示生成。通过将哈密顿量识别为相对于参考 KMS（真空）态的尖锐子区域的模哈密顿量，该代数转变为 III$_1$ 型因子。他们借助“开闭弦对偶”引入一个由位于不动点处的标量场构成的非平凡“涌现中心”，以分析涌现希尔伯特空间的结构。最后，他们在该模量子化框架下计算边界关联函数，并与 BTZ 黑洞的精确 Hartle-Hawking 关联函数进行比较。

Result: The modular quantization reproduces the exact Hartle-Hawking correlator of the smooth BTZ black hole in the strict semiclassical limit ($G_N \\to 0$). At finite Newton constant $G_N$, the dual description becomes intrinsically non-smooth, exhibiting both a stretched horizon and a boundary UV cutoff. The exact correlator is also recovered directly from vacuum correlators within the modular quantization framework. Incorporating gravity via AdS/CFT—by including the emergent center—alters the boundary correlators such that the smooth horizon is replaced by a stretched horizon containing explicit microstructure.
\n\n
该模量子化在严格的半经典极限（$G_N \\to 0$）下重现了光滑 BTZ 黑洞的精确 Hartle-Hawking 关联函数。在有限牛顿常数 $G_N$ 下，对偶描述本质上是非光滑的，同时表现出拉伸视界和边界紫外截断。精确关联函数也可直接从模量子化框架内的真空关联函数中复现。通过 AdS/CFT 引入引力——即包含涌现中心——会改变边界关联函数，使得光滑视界被包含显式微观结构的拉伸视界所取代。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that modular quantization of a single holographic CFT, inspired by background-independent quantum gravity, provides a consistent algebraic framework to capture non-perturbative black hole features. The transition from type-I to type-III$_1$ von Neumann algebras reflects the emergence of thermality and horizon physics. Crucially, smooth geometric horizons are effective only in the semiclassical limit; at finite $G_N$, quantum gravity effects manifest as a stretched horizon with microstructure, encoded in an emergent center linked to fixed points of the modular flow. This supports the view that spacetime geometry and horizons are emergent from quantum information-theoretic structures in the boundary theory.
\n\n
本文表明，受背景无关量子引力启发的单全息 CFT 的模量子化提供了一个一致的代数框架，可用于捕捉黑洞的非微扰特征。从 I 型到 III$_1$ 型冯·诺依曼代数的转变反映了热性和视界物理的涌现。关键在于，光滑几何视界仅在半经典极限下有效；在有限 $G_N$ 下，量子引力效应表现为带有微观结构的拉伸视界，其信息编码于与模流不动点相关的“涌现中心”中。这支持了时空几何与视界是从边界理论中的量子信息结构中涌现的观点。

Abstract: Witten recently proposed a background-independent algebraic framework for quantum gravity, wherein an observer endowed with a Hamiltonian defines a diffeomorphism invariant worldline algebra manifested by the modified Hamiltonian constraint. In the semiclassical limit, this construction admits a lift to a von Neumann algebra acting on a Hilbert space defined by geodesic in a fixed background. Motivated by this, we revisit quantization of certain class of deformed CFT Hamiltonian on a cylinder to capture non-perturbative aspects of black holes. We construct a type-I Von-Neuman algebra by imposing conformal boundary conditions on cut-offs near fixed points of Hamiltonian flow, acting on a GNS Hilbert space built from highest-weight representation of `emergent modular Virasoro algebra'. Upon identifying the Hamiltonian with the modular Hamiltonian of a sharp subregion associated to a fixed reference KMS (vacuum) state, the algebra changes to type-III$_{1}$ factor. We also discuss the structure of emergent Hilbert spaces using `open-closed string' duality after incorporating an emergent non-trivial center made out of scalars at fixed points. We further employ this modular quantization of a single holographic CFT to demonstrate how the boundary limit of exact Hartle-Hawking correlator of smooth BTZ background emerge in the strict semiclassical limit in an alternative dual description, while at finite $G_{N}$, the corresponding description is intrinsically non-smooth, featuring both a stretched horizon and a boundary cutoff. The exact correlator has also been precisely reproduced from the vacuum correlators in modular quantization. We further discuss the effect of incorporating gravity by including the center via AdS/CFT on boundary correlators, for which the description of a smooth horizon is replaced by a (stretched) horizon containing explicit microstructures embedded within it.

Result: In the $c \\to 0$ limit, the local U(1) gauge symmetry degenerates: the Gauss law ceases to be a constraint on initial data and becomes a conservation law, thereby freeing the longitudinal gauge mode as a new physical d.o.f., raising the count from $2(d-1)$ to $2d$. A no-go theorem is established: under a single-mode KK-like ansatz, no field rescaling can simultaneously preserve nontrivial dynamics and maintain a first-class Gauss law due to a structural mismatch between velocity-type and constraint-type terms in the action. Abstract: In this work, we apply deformed light-cone null reduction method to a complex Maxwell theory in a manifestly gauge-invariant formulation. We show that the local U(1) gauge structure degenerates in the $c\to 0$ limit: the Gauss law constraint reduces from a restriction on initial data to a conservation law, releasing the longitudinal gauge mode as an independent degree of freedom (d.o.f). Rather than representing the Carrollian electrodynamics derived via group contraction, the free complex scalar theory that emerges is merely an artifact of the truncation procedure at $c\to0$.

Abstract: Motivated by earlier approaches, we develop a worldsheet framework for computing correlation functions in the single trace $T \overline{T}$-deformed tensionless AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ duality. We construct deformed physical vertex operators and compute their tree-level two-point functions exactly. We discuss the relation of our results to previous proposals for $T \overline{T}$-deformed two-point functions obtained from alternative worldsheet approaches, JT gravity, and perturbative field theory computations.

*Fernando M. Belchior,Roberto V. Maluf*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper constructs a nonlocal extension of the Rarita-Schwinger theory for spin-$3/2$ fermions using two types of analytic form factors—scalar $f(\Box)$ and Dirac-operator $f(\slashed{\partial})$—and demonstrates that the unphysical spin-$1/2$ modes remain non-dynamical, preserving consistency at the free level while modifying dispersion relations.

本文构建了自旋-$3/2$费米子的Rarita-Schwinger理论的一种非局域推广，采用两类解析型形状因子——标量型 $f(\Box)$ 与狄拉克算符型 $f(\slashed{\partial})$——并证明非物理的自旋-$1/2$ 模式保持非动力学，从而在自由层级维持理论一致性，同时修改色散关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The local Rarita-Schwinger theory suffers from well-known issues such as the presence of unphysical spin-$1/2$ degrees of freedom and potential inconsistencies when coupled to interactions. Nonlocal modifications offer a way to address these problems by softening UV behavior while maintaining gauge invariance and causality. The motivation is to explore whether nonlocality can preserve the desirable features of the free theory—particularly the decoupling of unphysical modes—while introducing modified but consistent dynamics.
\n\n
局域的Rarita-Schwinger理论存在众所周知的问题，例如非物理的自旋-$1/2$ 自由度以及在引入相互作用时可能出现的不一致性。非局域修正提供了一种通过软化紫外（UV）行为同时保持规范不变性和因果性的途径。本文旨在探索非局域性是否能在保留自由理论优良特性（尤其是非物理模态的解耦）的同时，引入修改但自洽的动力学。

Method: The authors construct a nonlocal action for spin-$3/2$ fields by inserting analytic form factors $f(\Box)$ or $f(\slashed{\partial})$ into the kinetic operator. For the massless case, they implement a covariant nonlocal gauge-fixing term that allows the propagator to be expressed directly via the spin-$3/2$ projector $\\Pi_{3/2}$. In the massive case, they verify that the standard Rarita-Schwinger constraints ($\\gamma^\\mu \\psi_\\mu = 0$, $\\partial^\\mu \\psi_\\mu = 0$) are preserved due to the analyticity of $f$, ensuring the spin-$1/2$ sector remains non-dynamical. They analyze the pole structure and derive modified dispersion relations, especially for exponential form factors like $f(z) = e^{-z/\\Lambda^2}$.
\n\n
作者通过在动能算符中插入解析型形状因子 $f(\Box)$ 或 $f(\slashed{\partial})$ 来构建自旋-$3/2$ 场的非局域作用量。在无质量情形下，他们引入一个协变的非局域规范固定项，使得传播子可直接用自旋-$3/2$ 投影算符 $\\Pi_{3/2}$ 表示。在有质量情形下，他们验证了由于 $f$ 的解析性，标准的Rarita-Schwinger约束（$\\gamma^\\mu \\psi_\\mu = 0$，$\\partial^\\mu \\psi_\\mu = 0$）得以保持，从而确保自旋-$1/2$ 扇区保持非动力学。他们分析了极点结构，并推导出修改后的色散关系，尤其针对指数型形状因子如 $f(z) = e^{-z/\\Lambda^2}$。

Result: For scalar form factors $f(\Box)$, the tensor-spinor structure of the propagator matches the local theory, but the pole equation becomes $p^2 f(-p^2) + m^2 = 0$, leading to an infinite set of poles if $f$ is entire and non-polynomial. For Dirac-operator form factors $f(\slashed{\partial})$, the physical spin-$3/2$ modes satisfy a nonlocal Dirac-type equation $f(i\\slashed{\\partial}) (i\\slashed{\\partial} - m) \\psi_\\mu = 0$, yielding explicitly solvable modified dispersion relations for exponential $f$. Crucially, the unphysical spin-$1/2$ modes do not acquire kinetic terms and remain constrained, avoiding ghosts at the free level.
\n\n
对于标量形状因子 $f(\Box)$，传播子的张量-旋量结构与局域理论一致，但极点方程变为 $p^2 f(-p^2) + m^2 = 0$，若 $f$ 为整函数且非多项式，则导致无穷多极点。对于狄拉克算符型形状因子 $f(\slashed{\partial})$，物理的自旋-$3/2$ 模满足非局域狄拉克型方程 $f(i\\slashed{\\partial}) (i\\slashed{\\partial} - m) \\psi_\\mu = 0$，对指数型 $f$ 可显式求解其修改后的色散关系。关键的是，非物理的自旋-$1/2$ 模未获得动能项，仍受约束，从而在自由层级避免鬼态。

Conclusion: The nonlocal Rarita-Schwinger theory with analytic form factors successfully maintains the decoupling of unphysical spin-$1/2$ degrees of freedom at the free level, offering a ghost-free framework with modified dispersion relations. Abstract: In this paper, one constructs a nonlocal extension of the Rarita-Schwinger theory for spin-$3/2$ fermions. Two classes of analytic form factors are considered: scalar form factors $f(\Box)$ and Dirac-operator form factors $f(\slashed{\partial})$. The massless theory is treated together with a covariant nonlocal gauge fixing, which allows the propagator to be written directly in terms of the spin-$3/2$ projector. In the massive theory, we show that the free Rarita-Schwinger constraints remain intact for analytic form factors, so that the unphysical spin-$1/2$ sector does not become dynamical. For $f(\Box)$ the tensor-spinor structure of the propagator is the same as in the local theory, while the pole equation is deformed by the scalar form factor. For $f(\slashed{\partial})$ the physical modes obey a nonlocal Dirac-type equation, leading to modified dispersion relations that can be written explicitly for exponential form factors. We discuss the conditions under which the construction is ghost-free at the free level and identify the natural limitations that must be addressed before interactions are introduced.

</details>


### [20] [Entanglement generation between field modes mediated by a fluctuating conducting wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12338)
*Luca Giovanni Cammarata,Tommaso Fazio,Roberto Passante,Lucia Rizzuto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates quantum entanglement between two one-dimensional massless scalar fields confined in adjacent sub-cavities separated by a quantum-mechanically fluctuating movable conducting plate. By modeling the system via a generalized Law Hamiltonian and applying second-order perturbation theory, the authors compute the ground state and reduced density matrices of the fields. They analytically evaluate the negativity for a pair of field modes and numerically compute the total multimode negativity, demonstrating that quantum fluctuations of the movable wall induce entanglement between the fields—unlike the fixed-wall case—and analyze its dependence on physical parameters such as wall mass, oscillation frequency, cavity geometry, and field mode frequencies.
\n\n
本文研究了由一个量子力学涨落的可移动导电板分隔的两个相邻子腔中的一维无质量标量场之间的量子纠缠。作者通过广义的Law Hamiltonian建模系统，并应用二阶微扰理论计算系统的基态和场的约化密度矩阵。他们解析计算了一对场模的negativity，并数值计算了总多模negativity，证明可移动壁的量子涨落会在两子腔场之间诱导出纠缠——这与固定壁情形截然不同——并分析了纠缠量对物理参数（如壁的质量、振荡频率、腔体几何结构和场模频率）的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how quantum mechanical motion of a boundary (here, a movable conducting plate with finite mass) influences quantum field correlations and entanglement across spatially separated regions. In standard Casimir setups with fixed boundaries, fields in disjoint cavities are unentangled. This work explores whether allowing the boundary itself to undergo quantum fluctuations—thereby coupling it dynamically to the quantum fields—can generate nontrivial entanglement between otherwise isolated field subsystems, offering insights into quantum vacuum structure, quantum optomechanics, and the interplay between geometry and entanglement in quantum field theory.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于理解边界（此处为具有有限质量的可移动导电板）的量子力学运动如何影响空间分离区域中量子场的相关性和纠缠。在具有固定边界的常规Casimir构型中，不相交腔体中的场是不纠缠的。本文探讨：若允许边界本身经历量子涨落——从而使其与量子场动态耦合——是否能在原本孤立的场子系统之间产生非平凡的纠缠，这为理解量子真空结构、量子光机系统以及量子场论中几何与纠缠的相互作用提供了新见解。

Method: The system is modeled using a generalized Law Hamiltonian that includes: (i) the harmonic oscillator Hamiltonian for the movable plate’s center-of-mass motion, (ii) the free Hamiltonians for two independent 1D massless scalar fields in each sub-cavity, and (iii) an effective interaction term arising from the position-dependent boundary condition due to the plate’s displacement $x$. Expanding the field modes in powers of $x$ and treating the coupling perturbatively, the authors compute the interacting ground state $|\\Omega\\rangle$ up to second order in perturbation theory. The reduced density operator for the fields in one sub-cavity is obtained by tracing out both the wall’s degrees of freedom and the field in the other cavity. Entanglement between a pair of modes (one from each cavity) is quantified via the negativity $\\mathcal{N}$, computed analytically from the covariance matrix of the bipartite Gaussian state. Total multimode negativity is evaluated numerically by diagonalizing the partially transposed reduced density matrix.
\n\n
该系统采用广义的Law Hamiltonian建模，包含：(i) 可移动板质心运动的谐振子Hamiltonian，(ii) 每个子腔中两个独立的一维无质量标量场的自由Hamiltonian，以及(iii) 由于板位移$x$导致的边界条件位置依赖性所产生的有效相互作用项。作者将场模按$x$展开，并以微扰方式处理耦合，在二阶微扰理论下计算相互作用系统的基态$|\\Omega\\rangle$。通过对壁的自由度及另一腔中场进行迹运算，得到单个子腔中场的约化密度算符。通过negativity $\\mathcal{N}$量化来自两个腔的一对模之间的纠缠，该量从双部分Gaussian态的协方差矩阵解析计算得出；总多模negativity则通过对部分转置后的约化密度矩阵进行数值对角化获得。

Result: The key results are: (1) The quantum fluctuations of the movable wall induce entanglement between field modes residing in different sub-cavities, as evidenced by nonzero negativity—both for a single mode pair and for the full multimode state. (2) In contrast, if the wall is fixed ($m \\to \\infty$ or $\\omega_0 \\to \\infty$), the negativity vanishes, confirming no entanglement exists in that limit. (3) The amount of entanglement depends non-monotonically on system parameters: it increases with decreasing wall mass $m$ and decreasing oscillation frequency $\\omega_0$ (i.e., larger positional uncertainty), peaks at intermediate values of the plate’s equilibrium position (relative to cavity lengths), and is sensitive to the resonance between field mode frequencies and the wall’s mechanical frequency. These dependencies reflect the competition between coupling strength and zero-point fluctuations of the wall.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 可移动壁的量子涨落在不同子腔中的场模之间诱导出纠缠，表现为单模对和全多模态的negativity均非零；(2) 相比之下，若壁固定（$m \\to \\infty$ 或 $\\omega_0 \\to \\infty$），negativity消失，证实该极限下无纠缠；(3) 纠缠量对系统参数呈非单调依赖：随壁质量$m$减小和振荡频率$\\omega_0$降低（即位置不确定性增大）而增强，在板平衡位置（相对于腔长）取中间值时达到峰值，且对场模频率与壁机械频率之间的共振敏感。这些依赖关系反映了耦合强度与壁零点涨落之间的竞争。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that quantum mechanical motion of a boundary can serve as a mediator of entanglement between otherwise causally disconnected quantum fields. This demonstrates a novel mechanism for generating vacuum entanglement through dynamical boundaries, extending beyond static Casimir scenarios. The results highlight the role of boundary quantum fluctuations as a resource for entanglement and suggest potential applications in quantum information protocols involving field modes in engineered cavities, as well as implications for fundamental questions about entanglement structure in quantum field theory with dynamical spacetime boundaries.
\n\n
本文得出结论：边界的量子力学运动可作为媒介，在原本因果分离的量子场之间传递纠缠。这展示了一种通过动力学边界产生真空纠缠的新机制，超越了静态Casimir情形。结果凸显了边界量子涨落作为纠缠资源的作用，并暗示其在涉及工程腔中场模的量子信息协议中的潜在应用，以及对具有动力学时空边界的量子场论中纠缠结构基本问题的启示。

Abstract: We consider a movable conducting plate of finite mass, between two fixed ones, whose mechanical degrees of freedom are treated quantum-mechanically and bound to its equilibrium position by a harmonic potential. The movable wall is thus subjected to quantum fluctuations of its position. This creates a system of two sub-cavities separated by the movable fluctuating plate, and two massless one-dimensional scalar fields, one in each sub-cavity. This system is described by an appropriate generalization of the Law Hamiltonian. The presence of the movable wall yields an effective plate-fields interaction, as well as an effective interaction between the field modes. We obtain, at the second order in perturbation theory, the ground state of the interacting system and the reduced density operator of the fields in each sub-cavity by tracing out the wall's degrees of freedom. We calculate the entanglement between two field modes, one in each cavity, by evaluating analytically the negativity; we then evaluate numerically also the total multimode negativity. Our results show that in both cases the fields in the two sub-cavities are entangled, in contrast to the case in which the wall is fixed in space. We discuss the amount of the field entanglement present as a function of relevant physical parameters of the system such as the mass and oscillation frequency of the movable wall, its distance from the fixed walls and the frequencies of the field modes considered.

*Giacomo Santoni,Francesco Scardino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper introduces replica energy ${\cal E}$ as a thermodynamic measure of nonextensivity, defined via the operator $(1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L)$ acting on $W = \log Z$. In quantum field theory (QFT), it extracts central charges, sphere free energy $F$, Euler anomaly $a$, and renormalized defect free energies; in $2+1$D, its $n \to 1$ limit yields the entropic $F$-function. 本文引入“副本能量”（replica energy）${\cal E}$ 作为非广延性（nonextensivity）的热力学度量，通过算符 $(1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L)$ 作用于 $W = \log Z$ 定义。该滤波器移除体积极限下的广延贡献，分离出对边界、拓扑、缺陷或长程相互作用敏感的普适次广延信息。在量子场论（QFT）中，它可提取中心荷、球面自由能 $F$、欧拉反常系数 $a$ 以及重整化后的缺陷自由能；在 $2+1$ 维中，其 $n \to 1$ 极限精确给出熵型 $F$-函数。该框架为有限尺寸修正、能隙相中的拓扑常数、分形子简并度及自引力热物质等真正非广延系统提供了统一的热力学语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard thermodynamics assumes extensivity: $\log Z$ scales linearly with system size. However, many physical systems—ranging from QFTs on curved spaces to systems with topological order, defects, or long-range forces like gravity—violate this assumption. The authors seek a unified thermodynamic framework to characterize such nonextensive contributions, which often encode universal physics (e.g., anomalies, entanglement entropy). Existing methods treat these cases separately; the paper aims to show that a single differential operator applied to the free energy can systematically isolate these universal subextensive terms.\n\n
标准热力学假设广延性：$\log Z$ 随系统尺寸线性标度。然而，许多物理系统——从弯曲时空上的量子场论到具有拓扑序、缺陷或引力等长程力的系统——都违背这一假设。作者寻求一个统一的热力学框架来刻画这类非广延贡献，因为它们通常编码普适物理（如反常、纠缠熵）。现有方法对这些情形分别处理；本文旨在证明，通过对自由能施加单一微分算符，可系统性地分离出这些普适的次广延项。

Method: The core method is the application of the differential operator $(1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L)$ to the free energy $W = \log Z$, defining the replica energy ${\cal E} = (1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L) W$. This operator annihilates homogeneous (extensive) terms of degree $d$ in system size $L$, leaving only subextensive contributions. The authors generalize this to higher-order filters to remove local counterterms in QFT partition functions on spheres or finite volumes. For replica geometries used in entanglement entropy calculations, the same filtering yields the renormalized defect free energy. They analyze various systems—including gapped topological phases, fracton models, and self-gravitating thermal matter—to demonstrate the universality of this approach.\n\n
核心方法是对自由能 $W = \log Z$ 应用微分算符 $(1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L)$，定义副本能量 ${\cal E} = (1 - \frac{1}{d} L \partial_L) W$。该算符消去系统尺寸 $L$ 的 $d$ 次齐次（广延）项，仅保留次广延贡献。作者将此推广至高阶滤波器，以消除球面或有限体积上量子场论配分函数中的局域抵消项。对于用于纠缠熵计算的副本几何，相同滤波原理给出重整化的缺陷自由能。他们分析了多种系统——包括能隙拓扑相、分形子模型和自引力热物质——以展示该方法的普适性。

Result: The replica energy ${\cal E}$ successfully isolates universal subextensive data: in even-dimensional CFTs, it extracts the Euler anomaly coefficient $a$; in odd dimensions, the sphere free energy $F$; and for replica manifolds, the renormalized defect free energy whose $n \to 1$ limit in $2+1$D equals the entropic $F$-function. The framework distinguishes between ordinary finite-size corrections (which are extensive and filtered out), topological constants in gapped phases (subextensive and retained), fracton ground-state degeneracies (scaling subextensively), and genuinely nonextensive systems like self-gravitating matter (where ${\cal E}$ remains size-dependent). This provides a thermodynamic diagnostic for renormalization-group irreversibility and defect universality.\n\n
副本能量 ${\cal E}$ 成功分离出普适的次广延数据：在偶维共形场论（CFT）中提取欧拉反常系数 $a$；在奇维中提取球面自由能 $F$；对于副本流形，则得到重整化缺陷自由能，其在 $2+1$ 维的 $n \to 1$ 极限等于熵型 $F$-函数。该框架区分了普通有限尺寸修正（广延，被滤除）、能隙相中的拓扑常数（次广延，被保留）、分形子基态简并度（次广延标度）以及真正非广延系统（如自引力物质，其中 ${\cal E}$ 仍依赖尺寸）。这为重整化群不可逆性和缺陷普适性提供了热力学判据。

Conclusion: Replica energy ${\cal E}$ provides a unifying thermodynamic language that connects statistical mechanics and quantum field theory by quantifying deviations from extensivity. It serves as a universal filter to extract physically meaningful, nonlocal, and universal quantities—such as central charges, anomaly coefficients, and renormalized defect free energies—while discarding trivial extensive or local contributions. This approach clarifies the thermodynamic nature of entanglement, topological order, and long-range interacting systems, and offers a new perspective on renormalization-group flow irreversibility through the lens of nonadditivity.\n\n
副本能量 ${\cal E}$ 通过量化对广延性的偏离，为统计力学与量子场论提供了统一的热力学语言。它作为一种普适滤波器，可提取具有物理意义的非局域普适量——如中心荷、反常系数和重整化缺陷自由能——同时剔除平凡的广延或局域贡献。该方法阐明了纠缠、拓扑序及长程相互作用系统的热力学本质，并通过非可加性（nonadditivity）视角为重整化群流的不可逆性提供了新见解。

Abstract: Extensive systems have a simple thermodynamic signature: the logarithm of the partition function scales homogeneously with the size of the system. We show that the failure of this scaling, measured by the replica energy ${\cal E}$, provides a useful bridge between statistical mechanics and quantum field theory. The associated differential operator $(1-\frac1d L\partial_L)$ removes the leading bulk contribution to $W=\log Z$ and isolates the part that is sensitive to boundaries, topology, defects, long-range forces, or other sources of nonadditivity. In quantum field theory this thermodynamic idea has two closely related uses. For ordinary finite-volume or spherical partition functions, suitable higher-order versions of the same filter remove local counterterms and extract universal fixed-point data such as the central charge, the sphere free energy $F$, and the Euler anomaly coefficient $a$. For replica geometries with entangling defects, the same filtering principle gives the renormalized defect free energy. In $2+1$ dimensions, its $n\to1$ limit is precisely the entropic $F$-function. We use this perspective to distinguish ordinary finite-size corrections, topology-dependent constants in gapped phases, subextensive fracton degeneracies, and genuinely nonextensive systems with long-range interactions such as self-gravitating thermal matter. Replica energy therefore offers a common thermodynamic language for additivity, defect free energies, and renormalization-group irreversibility.

### [22] [Tripartite Entanglement in $e^+ e^- \to t \bar{t} Z$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11296)
