TL;DR: This paper studies multipartite entanglement in the $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ process at a future lepton collider by reconstructing the full $12\\times12$ spin density matrix of the final-state particles (top, antitop, and $Z$ boson) and quantifying entanglement using various negativity measures. Genuine tripartite and collective (one-to-other) entanglement dominate over pairwise entanglement, and all entanglement signatures degrade with increased phase space integration. The analysis suggests that collective entanglement is measurable at a high-luminosity polarized lepton collider operating at $\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV, while detecting genuine multipartite entanglement remains challenging without improved analysis strategies.\n\n该论文研究了未来轻子对撞机上 $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ 过程中的多体纠缠，通过重建末态粒子（顶夸克、反顶夸克和 $Z$ 玻色子）的完整 $12\\times12$ 自旋密度矩阵，并利用多种负性度量来量化纠缠。真正的三体纠缠和集体（one-to-other）纠缠主导了成对纠缠，且所有纠缠信号随着相空间积分程度的增加而减弱。分析表明，在 $\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV 的高亮度极化轻子对撞机上，集体纠缠是可以测量的，而探测真正的多体纠缠在没有改进分析策略的情况下仍然具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multipartite entanglement represents a uniquely quantum correlation that cannot be reduced to combinations of bipartite entanglement and is essential for understanding the full quantum structure of composite systems. High-energy processes like $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ offer a natural setting to probe such correlations because the spins of the final-state particles form a tripartite Hilbert space $\\mathscr{H}=\\mathbb{C}^{2}\\otimes\\mathbb{C}^{2}\\otimes\\mathbb{C}^{3}$. The motivation is to establish whether multipartite entanglement can be observed in realistic collider environments and to develop a general framework applicable to any mixed tripartite spin state in particle physics.\n\n多体纠缠是一种无法简化为双体纠缠组合的独特量子关联，对于理解复合系统的完整量子结构至关重要。像 $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ 这样的高能过程为探测此类关联提供了天然场景，因为末态粒子的自旋构成了一个三体希尔伯特空间 $\\mathscr{H}=\\mathbb{C}^{2}\\otimes\\mathbb{C}^{2}\\otimes\\mathbb{C}^{3}$。其动机在于确定是否可以在现实的对撞机环境中观测到多体纠缠，并发展一个适用于粒子物理中任意混合三体自旋态的通用框架。

Method: The authors start from Standard Model helicity amplitudes for $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ and construct the full $12\\times12$ spin density matrix $\\rho$ of the final-state system. They evaluate three entanglement measures: (i) one-to-one negativities (bipartite between each pair), (ii) one-to-other negativities (bipartite split between one particle and the other two), and (iii) the genuine multipartite negativity, which detects entanglement not reducible to any bipartition. These are computed at three levels of phase space inclusiveness: fully differential, partially integrated (over some kinematic variables), and fully integrated. Quantum tomography in the fully leptonic decay channel is assumed to access spin information, with collider parameters set to $\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV and high luminosity.\n\n作者从 $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ 的标准模型螺旋度振幅出发，构建末态系统的完整 $12\\times12$ 自旋密度矩阵 $\\rho$。他们评估了三种纠缠度量：(i) one-to-one 负性（每对粒子之间的双体纠缠），(ii) one-to-other 负性（一个粒子与其余两个粒子之间的双体划分），以及 (iii) genuine multipartite negativity（检测不可约于任何双体划分的真正多体纠缠）。这些度量在三种相空间包含程度下计算：全微分、部分积分（对某些运动学变量）和完全积分。假设通过全轻子衰变道进行量子层析以获取自旋信息，对撞机参数设为 $\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV 和高亮度。

Result: The analysis reveals that genuine multipartite and one-to-other entanglement are stronger than pairwise (one-to-one) entanglement across all phase space integration levels. All entanglement measures decrease as more kinematic information is integrated out, indicating that spin-momentum correlations carry significant entanglement content. Under realistic experimental conditions ($\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV, polarized beams, high luminosity), the collective (one-to-other) entanglement is statistically observable via quantum tomography in the fully leptonic channel, whereas genuine multipartite entanglement falls below current detection thresholds without further optimization of observables or event selection.\n\n分析表明，在所有相空间积分层次上，真正的多体纠缠和 one-to-other 纠缠均强于成对（one-to-one）纠缠。随着更多运动学信息被积分掉，所有纠缠度量均下降，表明自旋-动量关联携带了显著的纠缠内容。在现实实验条件（$\\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV、极化束流、高亮度）下，通过全轻子道的量子层析可以统计观测到集体（one-to-other）纠缠，而真正的多体纠缠在未进一步优化观测量或事例选择的情况下低于当前探测阈值。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that $e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ is a promising process for probing multipartite quantum entanglement in high-energy physics. It provides a general mixed-state framework for characterizing tripartite spin entanglement using negativity-based measures, applicable beyond this specific process. While collective entanglement is within reach of future lepton colliders, observing genuine tripartite entanglement will require refined analysis techniques, better observables, or higher statistics. This work bridges quantum information concepts with collider phenomenology, opening new avenues for testing foundational aspects of quantum mechanics in particle physics.\n\n该研究表明，$e^+e^-\\to t\\bar{t}Z$ 是在高能物理中探测量子多体纠缠的一个有前景的过程。它提供了一个基于负性度量的通用混合态框架，用于刻画三体自旋纠缠，可推广至其他过程。虽然集体纠缠在未来轻子对撞机的探测能力范围内，但观测真正的三体纠缠将需要更精细的分析技术、更好的观测量或更高统计量。这项工作将量子信息概念与对撞机唯象学相结合，为在粒子物理中检验量子力学基础开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement is a uniquely quantum form of correlation that captures collective properties of a composite quantum state beyond those encoded in its bipartite subsystems. We investigate this phenomenon in the process $e^+e^-\to t\bar tZ$ at a future lepton collider, where the final state spins span the tripartite Hilbert space $\mathscr{H}=\mathbb{C}^{2}\otimes\mathbb{C}^{2}\otimes\mathbb{C}^{3}$. Starting from the Standard Model helicity amplitudes, we reconstruct the full $12\times 12$ spin density matrix and characterise its entanglement structure through one-to-one negativities, one-to-other negativities, and the genuine multipartite negativity, evaluated at three increasingly inclusive levels of phase space integration. Pairwise entanglement is generally suppressed relative to the collective (one-to-other) and the genuine multipartite entanglement, and all measures decrease as more kinematic information is integrated out. Assuming quantum tomography in the fully leptonic decay channel at $\sqrt{s}=1$ TeV, we find that the collective entanglement is accessible at a realistic high-luminosity polarised lepton collider, while a direct observation of genuine multipartite entanglement is challenging and would benefit from further optimisation of the event analysis and observable choice. The study establishes $e^+e^-\to t\bar tZ$ as an attractive laboratory for probing multipartite entanglement in high-energy collisions and provides a general mixed state framework that applies to any tripartite spin system.

Method: The authors extend the quantum field-theoretical description of neutrino propagation by incorporating coherent forward scattering in matter within the EFT framework. Analytical expressions for oscillation probabilities are obtained for medium-baseline reactor setups, and a likelihood analysis is performed on JUNO data to constrain Wilson coefficients of the leading EFT operators.

作者在有效场论（EFT）框架内扩展了中微子传播的量子场论描述，纳入了物质中的相干前向散射效应。他们推导出控制中微子演化的有效哈密顿量，其中包含诱导非标准相互作用的维度-6算符。该哈密顿量随后与密度矩阵演化方程建立联系。针对中基线反应堆实验构型，获得了振荡概率的解析表达式，并对JUNO数据进行似然分析，以约束主导EFT算符的Wilson系数。

本文推导出了在存在维度-6 EFT算符和物质效应情况下中微子振荡观测量的闭合表达式。将其应用于JUNO实验，首次直接限制了涉及电子与夸克的半轻子四费米子算符的Wilson系数，转化为对非标准相互作用参数 $\\epsilon_{e\\alpha}^{uV}, \\epsilon_{e\\alpha}^{dV}$ 的约束，精度达到 $\\mathcal{O}(0.1)$–$\\mathcal{O}(1)$ 量级，与其他当前限制具有可比性。

Conclusion: The work establishes a consistent EFT-based formalism for neutrino oscillations with matter effects, bridging high-energy theory and experimental phenomenology. It demonstrates the feasibility of applying this framework to real reactor neutrino data, paving the way for future global EFT analyses across different neutrino sources and baselines.

本工作建立了包含物质效应的中微子振荡的一致性EFT形式体系，架起了高能理论与实验现象学之间的桥梁。它展示了将该框架应用于真实反应堆中微子数据的可行性，为未来跨不同中微子源和基线的全局EFT分析铺平了道路。

Abstract: We contribute to the systematic analysis of New Physics effects in neutrino experiments using Effective Field Theory (EFT) methods. We review and extend the quantum field-theoretical formalism for generic neutrino interactions, discussing the inclusion of matter effects and deriving the connection with the density matrix formalism. On the phenomenological side, we apply this framework for the first time to medium-baseline reactor neutrino experiments. We derive analytical expressions for the relevant oscillation observables and perform a first EFT analysis of the recent JUNO dataset, extracting bounds on the leading non-standard interaction parameters.

### [24] [Direct Detection of Millicharged Particles from Supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11310)
*Yanou Cui,Fengyi Li,Xiaolin Qi,Ian M. Shoemaker,Yu-Dai Tsai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper proposes using terrestrial detectors (XENONnT, JUNO, DUNE, Hyper-Kamiokande) to search for millicharged particles (mCPs) produced in core-collapse supernovae. Due to their tiny electric charge ($\varepsilon$) and mass, mCPs arrive at Earth later than SN neutrinos, creating a clean time window for detection via electron-recoil signals. For $\varepsilon = 10^{-9}$ and masses between sub-MeV and MeV, the expected signal exceeds 10 events per year, potentially improving SN cooling bounds on $\varepsilon$ by an order of magnitude.
\n\n
本文提出利用地面探测器（XENONnT、JUNO、DUNE、Hyper-Kamiokande）搜寻核心坍缩超新星（core-collapse supernovae）中产生的millicharged particles（mCPs）。由于其微小的电荷（$\varepsilon$）和质量，mCPs 比超新星中微子更晚到达地球，从而在中微子信号之后形成一个干净的时间窗口，可通过电子反冲信号进行探测。对于 $\varepsilon = 10^{-9}$ 且质量在 sub-MeV 到 MeV 范围内的情形，预期每年可探测到超过 10 个事例，有望将超新星冷却对 $\varepsilon$ 的限制提升近一个数量级。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that a TeV-scale $U(1)_{B-L}$ model with an inverse-seesaw mechanism can unify the origins of neutrino mass, dark matter, and the baryon asymmetry in a testable manner. The tight correlation between cosmological observables and collider phenomenology provides a powerful synergy: next-generation muon colliders can directly probe the same physics responsible for the relic density and leptogenesis, offering a unique opportunity to verify the fundamental origin of these cosmic puzzles within a single theoretical framework.

本工作表明，具有 inverse-seesaw 机制的 TeV 能标 $U(1)_{B-L}$ 模型能够以可检验的方式统一中微子质量、暗物质和重子不对称性的起源。宇宙学观测量与对撞机现象学之间的紧密关联提供了强大的协同效应：下一代缪子对撞机可直接探测导致剩余密度和轻子生成的相同物理机制，为在单一理论框架内验证这些宇宙学谜题的根本起源提供了独特机遇。

Abstract: We investigate a predictive and testable framework in which dark matter (DM), heavy-neutrino dynamics, and the BAU originate from correlated interactions within a local $U(1)_{B-L}$ extension of the SM. Unlike conventional $B-L$ constructions based on the type-I seesaw, we employ an inverse-seesaw mechanism realized through a sterile fermion $S_{1}$ and a complex scalar field $φ$, whose vacuum expectation value simultaneously generates the masses of the heavy neutrinos $N_{1,2}$ and the Majorana DM fermion $χ$ via Yukawa couplings. The small lepton-number-violating parameter induced by a higher-dimensional operator leads to naturally light active neutrinos together with TeV-scale heavy neutrinos and sizable active-sterile mixing, yielding distinctive collider signatures unavailable in minimal $B-L$ models. The relic abundance of $χ$ is governed by annihilation channels mediated by the same scalar and gauge interactions, producing a direct and model-specific correlation between successful leptogenesis and the observed DM relic density. A combined parameter-space analysis incorporating neutrino oscillation data, lepton-flavor-violating processes, direct-detection limits, and collider bounds on $N_{1,2}$ and $Z^\prime$ reveals a narrow yet robust region consistent with all these constraints. Representative benchmark points in this region are examined at a future multi-TeV muon collider. Heavy-neutrino production through electroweak processes yields striking signatures in the dilepton plus missing energy ($2\ell + E\!\!\!/_T$) and single-lepton plus di-jet plus missing energy ($1\ell + 2j + E\!\!\!/_T$) final states. These channels demonstrate that next-generation muon colliders offer a powerful and complementary probe of the inverse-seesaw origin of neutrino masses, the DM relic density, and the TeV-scale leptogenesis within such an extended $U(1)_{B-L}$ framework.

Abstract: We investigate whether a minimal $\mathbb{Z}_4$-symmetric fermion-scalar extension of the Standard Model can simultaneously realise viable dark matter, a strong electroweak phase transition, and a stochastic gravitational-wave signal. The model contains a real scalar singlet and a Dirac fermion, allowing thermal two-component dark matter, mixed WIMP-FIMP histories, and an effectively fermionic relic abundance generated by scalar decays. 作者通过在有限密度量子场论中计算轴矢流的散度和赝标量密度来显式计算轴矢反常，并分析反常Ward恒等式，识别出介质依赖项之间的抵消。随后，他们利用线性响应理论和轴矢流关联函数研究手征磁效应，并通过费米速度引入费米液体效应。最后，他们将该形式体系映射到轴子电动力学，其中轴子场提供有效的轴矢化学势。

Abstract: We explicitly compute the axial anomaly in dense matter and show that its form remains unchanged from that in vacuum, even in the massless limit. This result follows from a subtle cancellation in the anomalous Ward identity between the medium-induced contributions to the divergence of the axial current and to the pseudoscalar density. The current is determined by the axial chemical potential and suppressed by the Fermi velocity, in agreement with anomalous axial-current correlation functions. We finally discuss applications to axion physics, where axion dark matter acts as an effective axial chemical potential.

本工作为多个观测到的 $D_s$ 共振态（超出已知的 $D_{s0}(2317)$ 和 $D_{s1}(2460)$）提供了自洽的分子态解释，强化了粲-奇异强子谱中的分子图像。这些结果为量化粲夸克与底夸克系统间的重夸克味对称性破缺效应提供了重要基准，并激励在 $K^{(*)}D^{(*)}$ 和 $K^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$ 系统中进一步开展对所预言态的实验搜寻。

Abstract: Motivated by the observed $KD^{(*)}$ molecular candidates $D_{s0}(2317)$ and $D_{s1}(2460)$, their bottom--strange counterparts, $K\bar{B}^{(*)}$ molecular states, are naturally expected, although not yet experimentally established. This discrepancy may reflect sizable heavy-quark flavor symmetry breaking, which introduces significant model uncertainties. Current studies of heavy-quark flavor symmetry breaking effects still exhibit strong parameter dependence, and further experimental input is required to constrain these effects, in particular regarding possible additional $K^{(*)}D^{(*)}$ and $K^{(*)}\bar{B}^{(*)}$ molecular states. In this work, we examine whether additional $K^{*}D^{(*)}$ molecular states can be identified among the observed $D_s$ resonances. Within the Gaussian expansion method, we solve the Schrödinger equation using $σ$, $ρ$, $ω$, $π$, and $η$ exchange potentials, systematically including $S$-wave and higher partial waves. We find that $D_{s1}(2700)$ can be interpreted as a pure $P$-wave $DK^{*}$ molecule, while $D_{s1}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$ are well described as $D^{*}K^{*}$ molecular states dominated by the $^{1}P_{1}$ and $^{5}P_{3}$ components, respectively. These results provide a new description of the charmed--strange spectrum and a useful benchmark for heavy-quark flavor symmetry breaking effects.

### [31] [Matching of perturbative and exponentiated initial state radiation corrections to $e^+e^-$-annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11742)

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper analyzes higher-order radiative corrections from initial state radiation (ISR) in $e^+e^-$ annihilation, presents numerical predictions for future colliders, estimates theoretical uncertainties, proposes a modified exponentiation scheme for photonic and non-singlet pair corrections, constructs a matching procedure with existing analytic results, and introduces a new DIS-like subtraction scheme.

本文分析了正负电子湮灭过程中由初态辐射（ISR）引起的高阶辐射修正，给出了未来对撞机能量下的数值预测，估算了现有结果的理论不确定性，提出了一种针对纯光子和非单态对修正的改进指数化方案，构建了与现有解析高阶计算的匹配方法，并讨论了一种新的类深度非弹性散射（DIS-like）减除方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future high-precision $e^+e^-$ colliders demand accurate treatment of initial state radiation (ISR), whose higher-order effects can significantly distort cross sections. Existing calculations either lack full exponentiation of mixed photonic and fermionic corrections or suffer from ambiguities in matching fixed-order and resummed results. A consistent framework with controlled uncertainties is needed for reliable phenomenology.

未来的高精度正负电子对撞机要求精确处理初态辐射（ISR），其高阶效应会显著扭曲截面。现有计算要么缺乏对混合光子与费米子修正的完整指数化，要么在固定阶与重求和结果的匹配中存在模糊性。为实现可靠的唯象学预测，亟需一个具有可控不确定性的自洽框架。

Method: The authors develop a modified exponentiation formalism that simultaneously treats pure photonic ($\\gamma$-only) and non-singlet fermion-pair ISR corrections. They match this resummed result to known fixed-order analytic calculations at $\\mathcal{O}(\\alpha^2)$ and beyond. Additionally, they propose a novel DIS-like subtraction scheme to isolate universal ISR contributions, improving factorization and reducing scheme dependence.

Abstract: We investigate $J/ψ$ photoproduction off the proton, $γp \to J/ψp$, to elucidate the nonexistence of hidden-charm pentaquark signals reported by the GlueX and CLAS12 experiments. Within a coupled-channel rescattering mechanism, we employ the transition amplitudes from a previous coupled-channel analysis that dynamically generates the $P_{c\bar{c}}$ states. We find that the rescattering contributions from the $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c$ channels -- indispensable for the formation of the $P_{c\bar{c}}$ resonances -- are about one order of magnitude smaller than those from $\bar{D}^{(*)}Λ_c$, since $g_{\bar{D}^{(*)}NΣ_c}$ is roughly five times smaller than $g_{\bar{D}^{(*)}NΛ_c}$. Since the $P_{c\bar{c}}$ resonances couple to the $J/ψN$ channel predominantly through the $\bar{D}^{(*)}Σ_c$ intermediate states, their suppression prevents the pentaquark signal from appearing in photoproduction. TL;DR: This paper presents a refined theoretical analysis of non-leptonic weak decays ${\cal B}_{cc} \\to {\\cal B}_c V$, incorporating both short-distance (via naive factorization) and long-distance contributions (via full evaluation of hadronic triangle loop integrals).   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of ${\\cal B}_{cc} \\to {\\cal B}_c V$ decays only computed the imaginary part of long-distance contributions using the Cutkosky cutting rule, neglecting the real part and thus missing essential strong phases required for CP violation. Method: The decay amplitudes are decomposed into short-distance and long-distance parts. Short-distance contributions are evaluated under the naive factorization hypothesis. Long-distance final-state interactions are modeled using hadronic triangle diagrams, and—unlike prior work—the full one-loop integrals are computed analytically or numerically to obtain both real and imaginary parts of the amplitudes. Model parameters (e.g., coupling constants, form factors) are constrained by existing experimental data. The total amplitude is then used to compute branching ratios, decay asymmetry parameters ($\\alpha$), and direct CP asymmetries $A_{CP}$ for Cabibbo-favored and singly Cabibbo-suppressed channels.

衰变振幅被分解为短程和长程部分。短程贡献在naive factorization假设下计算。长程末态相互作用通过强子三角图建模，与以往工作不同的是，本文完整计算了一圈积分（解析或数值），以获得振幅的实部和虚部。模型参数（如耦合常数、形状因子）由现有实验数据约束。总振幅随后用于计算Cabibbo允许和单Cabibbo压低道的分支比、衰变不对称参数（$\\alpha$）以及直接CP不对称度 $A_{CP}$。

Abstract: We study the non-leptonic weak decays of doubly charmed baryons (${\cal B}_{cc}$) into singly charmed baryons (${\cal B}_c$) and vector mesons ($V$), denoted as ${\cal B}_{cc}\to{\cal B}_{c}V$. These provide the nontrivial strong phases essential for CP violation. The model parameters are determined using experimental data. With this improved calculation method, we predict the branching ratios and decay asymmetry parameters for various decay channels, as well as $CP$ violations for short-distance dominated and singly Cabibbo-suppressed channels. This strengthens our theoretical framework for future study of doubly charmed baryons. Certain decays, primarily driven by long-distance effects, have been calculated; their observation in future experiments could help clarify the role of final-state interactions in charm baryon decays. Therefore, our calculation of ${\cal B}_{cc}\to{\cal B}_{c}V$ provides crucial predictions for branching ratios, decay asymmetry parameters, and $CP$ violation, which are essential for guiding experimental study at LHCb.

### [34] [Factorizing quarkonium LDMEs and TMDSTFs using effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12175)
*Marston Copeland*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper employs effective field theory (EFT) techniques—specifically a hybrid vNRQCD/pNRQCD Lagrangian with a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation—to decouple soft and ultrasoft gluon dynamics from heavy quark fields at leading order in velocity power-counting. This yields a re-factorization of NRQCD quarkonium production matrix elements into products of the wave-function at the origin and universal vacuum correlators of chromo-electric/magnetic fields, thereby verifying known relations among S-wave Long-Distance Matrix Elements (LDMEs) and deriving new constraints for TMD soft transition functions.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to better understand and constrain the nonperturbative Long-Distance Matrix Elements (LDMEs) in NRQCD factorization for quarkonium production. The authors aim to exploit the symmetries and separation of scales inherent in EFTs like pNRQCD to derive rigorous, model-independent relationships among LDMEs, especially extending such insights to the transverse momentum dependent (TMD) factorization framework where analogous nonperturbative objects (TMD soft transition functions) lack sufficient theoretical constraints.
\n\n
动机源于对NRQCD因子化中非微扰Long-Distance Matrix Elements（LDMEs）进行更好理解和约束的需求。现有的LDME提取存在较大不确定性，且在不同过程中表现出明显的不一致性。作者旨在利用pNRQCD等有效场论中固有的对称性和尺度分离，推导出LDME之间严格且与模型无关的关系，特别是将此类洞见拓展至横向动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架，其中类似的非微扰对象（TMD软跃迁函数）缺乏足够的理论约束。

Method: The method involves constructing a hybrid effective Lagrangian combining velocity-dependent NRQCD (vNRQCD) and potential NRQCD (pNRQCD). A Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation is applied to decouple the soft and ultrasoft gluon fields from the heavy quark bilinears. Through field redefinitions, the soft and ultrasoft sectors are integrated out, leading to a re-factorized form of the quarkonium production operators. At leading order in the velocity expansion ($v \\ll 1$), these operators reduce to color-singlet composite fields whose matrix elements factorize into the square of the wave-function at the origin $|\\psi(0)|^2$ and universal vacuum expectation values (VEVs) of time-ordered products of chromo-electric ($E^a$) and chromo-magnetic ($B^a$) fields, e.g., $\\langle 0 | T\\{ E^a(t) E^b(0) \\} | 0 \\rangle$.
\n\n
方法包括构建一个结合速度依赖NRQCD（vNRQCD）和势NRQCD（pNRQCD）的混合有效拉格朗日量。通过应用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换，将软模与超软模胶子场从重夸克双线性项中解耦。借助场重新定义，积分掉软模与超软模部分，从而得到夸克偶素产生算符的重因子化形式。在速度展开（$v \\ll 1$）的领头阶下，这些算符简化为色单态复合场，其矩阵元可因子化为原点波函数模平方$|\\psi(0)|^2$与色电场（$E^a$）和色磁场（$B^a$）时间序乘积的普适真空期望值（VEVs），例如$\\langle 0 | T\\{ E^a(t) E^b(0) \\} | 0 \\rangle$。

Result: The key results are: (1) A rigorous derivation confirming previously conjectured relations among the S-wave quarkonium LDMEs using pNRQCD symmetry arguments; (2) A novel factorization formula for quarkonium production matrix elements in terms of $|\\psi(0)|^2$ and universal gluonic correlators; (3) New, powerful model-independent relationships for the nonperturbative TMD soft transition functions, significantly reducing their functional freedom and providing stringent constraints for phenomenological applications in TMD factorization.

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that systematic EFT methods can uncover hidden universal structures in quarkonium production. By cleanly separating scales and exploiting field redefinitions, it provides a first-principles foundation for LDME universality and extends these insights to the TMD regime. The derived constraints on TMD soft transition functions represent a major step toward a more predictive and consistent theoretical description of quarkonium production at low transverse momentum.

本工作表明，系统性的有效场论方法能够揭示夸克偶素产生中隐藏的普适结构。通过清晰地分离尺度并利用场重新定义，为LDME的普适性提供了第一性原理基础，并将这些洞见拓展至TMD区域。所导出的对TMD软跃迁函数的约束，是迈向对低横向动量下夸克偶素产生过程实现更具预言性和一致性的理论描述的重要一步。

Abstract: We use effective field theory to factorize production matrix elements that appear in the NRQCD framework for quarkonium cross sections. By applying a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation and appropriate field redefinitions, we show that the soft and ultrasoft sectors of NRQCD can be decoupled from the heavy quark and antiquark fields in a hybrid vNRQCD/pNRQCD Lagrangian at leading order in the velocity power-counting. This enables us to re-factorize quarkonium production matrix elements in terms of matrix elements of color-singlet composite fields, which we can write as the wave-function at the origin, and state independent vacuum correlators of chromo-electric and chromo-magnetic gluon fields. This approach verifies powerful relationships between the LDMEs of different S-wave quarkonia originally derived using pNRQCD. Additionally, it allows us to derive new relationships for the production matrix elements used in the transverse momentum dependent factorization (TMD) framework, known as TMD soft transition functions, providing a much stronger set of constraints on these nonperturbative operators. This work significantly advances our understanding of quarkonium production, particularly in the TMD framework.

Method: The paper applies femtoscopic correlation techniques—commonly used in heavy-ion physics to extract source sizes from two-particle momentum correlations—to inclusive photonuclear processes. Result: Femtoscopy is shown to be capable of disentangling nuclear shadowing from gluon saturation effects by probing the spatial structure of the emitting source at the sub-fermi scale. The technique reveals sensitivity to the transverse size of the photon-induced dipole and to localized enhancements in gluon density (hot spots), offering a new observable to constrain saturation models.

研究表明，femtoscopy可通过探测亚费米尺度下发射源的空间结构，有效区分nuclear shadowing与gluon saturation效应。该技术对光子诱导dipole的横向尺寸及gluon密度局域增强（hot spots）具有灵敏度，为约束saturation模型提供了新观测量。

Conclusion: Femtoscopy provides a powerful and direct probe of the initial-state geometry in photonuclear collisions, with unique sensitivity to gluon saturation and sub-nucleonic structures. Its application at the LHC and future EIC can significantly advance our understanding of high-density QCD matter and the spatial organization of gluons in nuclei.

femtoscopy为光核碰撞中的初态几何结构提供了强大而直接的探针，对gluon saturation和亚核子结构具有独特灵敏度。其在LHC及未来EIC上的应用将显著推进我们对高密度QCD物质及原子核内gluon空间构型的理解。

Abstract: We present femtoscopy as a new way to search for saturated gluonic matter in inclusive photonuclear processes, such as inclusive ultraperipheral collisions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) and the inclusive photonuclear reactions at the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). As the femtoscopic approaches are sensitive to the space-time structure of the particle emitting source, they are ideal in providing insights also about the initial stage of the collision, where the gluon distributions may impact the effective size of the dipole the quasireal photons oscillate into. This technique demonstrates its capabilities in isolating nuclear shadowing and gluon saturation effects. Finally, we show how a femtoscopic approach is highly sensitive to sub-fermi scale structures typically observed in ultraperipheral collisions, such as gluonic hot spots.

Abstract: We critically examine the expectation that in a fundamental quantum field theory, dimensionless couplings in the Lagrangian density should all be of order unity. For a theory with 20 IID unit normal couplings, the probability that the magnitude of the ratio of two of them is greater than 100 is 0.29, for example.   <summary>Details</summary>

动机源于探索超越标准WIMP范式的、理论上合理且现象学可行的暗物质场景。“隔离型暗物质”框架允许暗物质与标准模型（SM）粒子的直接耦合被压低，从而规避严格的直接探测限制，同时仍可通过新媒介粒子的动力学机制获得正确的剩余丰度。引入一个带 $Z_4$ 电荷的马约拉纳费米子和一个获得真空期望值的新标量场，构成了一个极小且紫外完备的理论框架，自然地解释了暗物质的稳定性与质量起源。理解此类模型是否仍具备可检验性——尤其是在对撞机上——对于指导未来的实验搜寻至关重要。

Abstract: We revisited the singlet fermionic dark matter model in this work, where a Majorana fermion $χ$ carrying $Z_4$ charge is assumed as the DM candidate. We focus on the heavy DM mass region, and our analysis indicates that the mixing angle does not necessarily need to be very small in the secluded dark matter scenario, which offers potential for probing such models in future collider experiments.

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether neutron capture information in Gadolinium-doped water Cherenkov detectors can statistically improve neutrino directionality reconstruction for Inverse Beta Decay (IBD) events, despite isotropic positron emission and neutron diffusion. Using Geant4 simulations, the authors demonstrate a potential statistical enhancement in directional sensitivity across neutrino energies from ~10 MeV to several hundred MeV, though practical gains depend on vertex resolution and detector performance.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In water Cherenkov detectors like Super-Kamiokande, Inverse Beta Decay (IBD) events produce positrons whose directions are nearly isotropic at low energies (e.g., supernova neutrinos), making it difficult to infer the incoming neutrino direction. With the addition of Gadolinium to enhance neutron capture efficiency, the possibility arises that the spatial correlation between the initial neutrino momentum and the final neutron capture position—despite neutron diffusion—could enable statistical reconstruction of neutrino directionality.

Method: The authors perform Monte Carlo simulations using Geant4 to model neutron propagation and capture in water following IBD interactions. They analyze the angular correlation between the incident neutrino direction and the vector from the IBD vertex to the neutron capture point, across a broad neutrino energy range (10 MeV to several hundred MeV). The study evaluates whether this correlation persists after accounting for realistic neutron diffusion and compares directional inference using only positron information versus combined positron–neutron data.

在掺钆水切伦科夫探测器中，中子俘获信息为IBD事例中微子方向性的统计重建提供了有前景的新途径。要实现这一潜力，需在顶点重建算法和探测器硬件方面取得进展，以获得足够的空间分辨率。因此，本工作推动了基于模拟的分析技术与硬件升级的进一步研发。

Abstract: One of the most important neutrino interactions is the Inverse Beta Decay (IBD). However, the IBD events typically carry no directional information in water Cherenkov detectors as the positron directions are mostly isotropic at low energies, such as those in supernova studies. As Gadolinium is being added to Super-Kamiokande, the improved neutron capture efficiency not only allows better background rejection, but the neutron capture information could potentially provide additional information that allows better event reconstruction. Due to neutron diffusion in water, event-by-event reconstruction is difficult. However, if the final neutron capture position is correlated with the initial neutrino momentum, it may be possible that neutrino directionality could be reconstructed statistically, with or without using the positron information. In this work, we use Geant4 to simulate neutron propagation in water. We show that in a wide range of neutrino energies from about 10 MeV to several hundred MeV, neutron capture information could statistically enhance the neutrino directionality, compared to positron-only inference, even with neutron diffusion considered. However, practical application of this technique depends crucially on detection effects, especially the vertex reconstruction resolutions. Our work therefore motivates developments of better reconstruction algorithms and techniques, as well as detector upgrades.

For quantum simulation, they examine Trotterization of the time-evolution operator, estimate the number of entangling and non-Clifford gates, and evaluate fermion-to-qubit encodings (e.g., Jordan-Wigner vs. more compact schemes) to minimize qubit and gate overhead.
\n\n
作者研究了一个简化的中微子气体模型，并比较了两种理论方法：(1) 包含非前向散射碰撞项的量子动力学方程；(2) 使用完整中微子-中微子相互作用哈密顿量的精确处理。他们分析了两种方法在特征时间尺度和渐近行为上的差异。对于量子模拟，他们考察了时间演化算符的Trotter分解，估算了纠缠门和非Clifford门的数量，并评估了费米子到量子比特的编码方案（如Jordan-Wigner编码与更紧凑的编码），以最小化量子比特和量子门的开销。

Result: The kinetic and many-body approaches yield quantitatively and qualitatively different evolutions: the former exhibits faster relaxation and distinct asymptotic states due to its treatment of collisions, while the latter retains coherent many-body entanglement. Quantum resource estimates show that simulating the truncated Hamiltonian requires fewer gates than typical high-energy physics problems but more than most quantum chemistry applications; the full Hamiltonian incurs higher costs. Efficient encodings reduce qubit count significantly, lowering overall circuit complexity.
\n\n
动力学方法与多体方法在演化上表现出定量和定性差异：前者由于其对碰撞项的处理而展现出更快的弛豫过程和不同的渐近态，而后者则保留了相干的多体纠缠。量子资源估算表明，模拟截断哈密顿量所需的门数少于典型的高能物理问题，但多于大多数量子化学应用；完整哈密顿量的模拟成本更高。高效的编码方案可显著减少所需量子比特数，从而降低整体线路复杂度。

Conclusion: A consistent description of neutrino evolution in dense environments requires going beyond standard kinetic theory to include genuine many-body quantum effects. While classically challenging, such simulations are potentially feasible on near-term quantum hardware, provided efficient fermion encodings and error-aware circuit design are employed. This work bridges neutrino physics, many-body quantum dynamics, and quantum computing.
\n\n
在致密环境中对中微子演化的自洽描述需要超越标准动力学理论，纳入真实的多体量子效应。尽管经典计算面临挑战，但在采用高效费米子编码和考虑误差的线路设计的前提下，此类模拟有望在近期量子硬件上实现。本工作连接了中微子物理、多体量子动力学与量子计算。

Abstract: Neutrino evolution in dense astrophysical environments is typically described either within a quantum kinetic framework, which neglects the build-up of multi-body correlations, or through simplified many-body calculations that allow significant entanglement to develop. In this work, we compare these two approaches in a simple neutrino-gas configuration, with particular emphasis on the role of non-forward scattering processes. These effects are incorporated either through a collision term in the kinetic description, or by considering the full neutrino-neutrino many-body Hamiltonian. We highlight differences between the two descriptions in both their characteristic timescales and asymptotic behavior. Motivated by the natural suitability of quantum computing for many-body calculations, we further investigate the non-classicality of neutrino evolution, discussing Trotter error scaling, along with the associated costs of constructing quantum circuits in terms of entangling gates and non-Clifford gates. We find that the resources needed for neutrino many-body evolution are on the low end of typical high-energy physics problems and on the mid to high end with respect to quantum chemistry problems. For the full Hamiltonian, resource requirements increase relative to the truncated version. We emphasize the importance of efficient fermion-to-qubit encodings, which are essential for reducing the substantial computational resources required for such simulations.

在 $k_{\max}=0.2\,h\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ 处，神经标记相对于经典标记将 $σ_8$ 的边缘化约束提升 $2.9\times$，$Ω_m$ 提升 $1.8\times$，在Fisher信息层面有效打破了 $Ω_m$–$σ_8$ 简并。它还将参数估计的均方误差（MSE）比最佳经典标记降低了 $1.45\times$。所学习的潜在表示与参数空间中宇宙学信息的主方向对齐。

Conclusion: The neural marking approach provides a powerful, interpretable generalization of marked statistics that recovers more cosmological information from the large-scale structure. By connecting morphological transformations to parameter inference through contrastive learning, it offers a promising path toward optimal summary statistics for future cosmological surveys.

神经标记方法为标记统计量提供了一种强大且可解释的推广，能够从大尺度结构中恢复更多宇宙学信息。通过对比学习将形态变换与参数推断联系起来，为未来宇宙学巡天提供了通向最优摘要统计量的可行路径。

Abstract: Recovering cosmological information beyond the power spectrum is a central goal for upcoming cosmological surveys, since late-time non-Gaussian signal in the matter density cannot be accessed through two-point statistics alone. Marked statistics fold part of this information back into the two-point level by reweighting the field with non-linear functions. We propose a neural marking scheme to generalize this process through a set of interpretable, physically motivated transformations that directly allow to interpret the gain in cosmological information at the morphological level. We employ a contrastive learning objective to align learnable marked summaries with the underlying cosmological parameters. At $k_{\max}=0.2\,h\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$, our neural mark tightens the marginalized constraint on $σ_8$ by $2.9\times$ and on $Ω_m$ by $1.8\times$ compared to classical marks, breaking the $Ω_m-σ_8$ degeneracy at the Fisher information level. It further reduces the parameter MSE across our cosmological parameter prior by $1.45\times$ over the best classical mark. The learned latent geometry aligns with the $Ω_m$ and $σ_8$ directions in parameter space, indicating that the contrastive objective recovers the dominant axes of cosmological information. Our approach opens the door to more powerful, interpretable summary statistics for cosmological inference.

Abstract: Parity-violating physics in the Universe can generate correlations between the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) $E$- and $B$-modes, but detecting such signals requires extremely accurate calibration of instruments. We describe a data-driven method to calibrate the relative polarisation angle between CMB experiments using cross-correlations of observations over a common sky region. Unlike standard self-calibration approaches, this method does not assume vanishing isotropic cosmic birefringence or primordial $EB$ correlations when estimating the relative misalignment angle, and therefore preserves sensitivity to parity-violating physics. If they can be calibrated to an uncertainty of $0.08^\circ$, as anticipated from the SO wire grid calibration system, we show that the SO Large Aperture Telescope and Planck could be calibrated to uncertainties of $0.10^\circ$ and $0.17^\circ$, respectively, at $\sim 145$ GHz. This approach relies on the availability of at least one well-calibrated instrument, and provides a complementary path to improving polarisation calibration across experiments, enabling more robust searches for parity-violating physics in the CMB, such as cosmic birefringence.

Conclusion: The integration of information theory, neural-network-enhanced data compression, and end-to-end simulation-based Bayesian inference enables unprecedented extraction of cosmological information from weak lensing data while rigorously accounting for systematics. Abstract: We present cosmological constraints from the Dark Energy Survey Year 3 (DES Y3) weak lensing data using hierarchical hybrid statistics within a Bayesian simulation-based inference framework that is based on the Gower Street simulations. To maximize the precision of the inference, we have developed a new, information-theory based, data compression of the weak lensing maps to just seven highly informative summary statistics. The hybrid scheme exploits the high information content of the power spectrum, compressing both the power spectrum and neural-based summaries that are designed to extract further information. Our simulation-based approach enables principled forward modelling of all major sources of systematic uncertainty and survey properties into realistic mock observations, including the survey mask, photometric redshift uncertainties, intrinsic galaxy alignments, multiplicative shear calibration bias, source galaxy clustering, non-Gaussian shape noise, and non-linear structure formation. The summary statistics are then used in a Bayesian simulation-based inference pipeline. The inference is validated through coverage tests and checks for robustness against baryonic feedback. Assuming a $w$CDM cosmology, our analysis yields $S_8 = 0.808 \pm 0.017$, $Ω_{\rm m} = 0.325 \pm 0.024$, and $w < -0.766$ (marginalized posterior 68 per cent credible intervals). This rigorous combination of information theory, physics- and neural network-based extreme data compression, and principled Bayesian analysis improves the figure of merit for $(Ω_{\rm m}, S_8, w)$ by 60 per cent over the previous state-of-the-art, and by almost a factor of 3 over two-point analyses of the same data. They are the most precise joint constraints on $(Ω_{\rm m}, S_8, w)$ from weak gravitational lensing data alone of any survey to date. We intend to apply this analysis to the more recent DES Y6 data.

再热动力学并非仅仅是后期细节，而是连接早期宇宙暴胀与晚期宇宙学观测的关键环节。研究表明，纳入具有物理动机的再热模型——特别是具有动态衰变率的模型——不仅能缓解 $H_0$ 张力等问题，还能显著提升对原初观测量（$n_s$、$r$）的预测精度。因此，一致的暴胀-再热建模对于从CMB数据中进行稳健的宇宙学推断至关重要。

Abstract: We develop a stochastic framework for the stationary spherical Jeans equation, motivated by the field-dependent nature of the gravitational coupling in scalar--tensor theories. Field-level inference (FLI) offers a promising route by operating directly on map-level data or learned summaries that preserve spatial correlations beyond the power spectrum.

非线性引力演化在宇宙密度场中留下了高阶统计和形态学信息，这些信息无法通过功率谱等两点统计量获取。随着下一代巡天即将提供高分辨率的弱引力透镜图，开发能够利用这些额外信息的推断框架变得至关重要。场级推断（Field-Level Inference, FLI）通过直接作用于地图级数据或保留超越功率谱的空间关联的学习摘要，提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Nonlinear structure growth generates higher-order correlations and morphological features in the cosmic density field that cannot be fully characterized by two-point statistics. Upcoming surveys will measure these features with greater precision, making it essential to develop methods capable of extracting as much cosmological information as possible from them. In this work, we compare FLI with power-spectrum-based inference using the same forward-modeling pipeline for generating weak lensing maps, with the goal of quantifying the gain from map-level analysis relative to two-point statistics. We perform this comparison with both implicit and explicit inference methods, using 8-million-parameter forward models based on Lagrangian perturbation theory and particle-mesh (PM) N-body evolution. The two FLI approaches yield closely consistent posteriors; this agreement, together with coverage tests confirming the calibration of the implicit analyses, gives us confidence in the recovered field-level constraints. Relative to the power-spectrum-based analyses, these results show significant gains in cosmological information, especially when small scales are included in the PM-based forward model. We then discuss the remaining challenges that must be addressed before PM-based explicit FLI can be applied to observational datasets.

Abstract: Quasars can serve as standard candles through an empirical scaling relation between their ultraviolet (UV) and X-ray luminosities. As high-redshift probes, it is critical to test whether this relation evolves with redshift. In this work, we reconstruct the Hubble diagram of the Pantheon+ sample using the deep learning--based LADDER algorithm and use it as a reference to investigate the quasar scaling relation. Our results, which are consistent with those from Gaussian process regression and narrow-bin analyses, show that the potentially contaminated sample at $z<0.7$ differs significantly from the $z>0.7$ sample; thus, it should be further screened or excluded when quasars are used as cosmological probes. We find that the scaling relation exhibits a non-linear redshift dependence that cannot be accounted for by a simple linear correction, and that this behavior is a feature of the current data sample rather than a consequence of cosmological model misspecification. To use quasars as standardizable candles, further modeling of the scaling relation and intrinsic dispersion, or more advanced data processing techniques, is required.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard weak lensing analyses based on two-point correlations are limited by their primary sensitivity to the degenerate parameter combination $S_8 = σ_8 (Ω_m/0.3)^{0.5}$, which hinders independent constraints on $Ω_m$ and other cosmological parameters. To overcome this limitation and extract more information from weak lensing surveys, higher-order (non-Gaussian) statistics—specifically third-order cosmic shear—are necessary to break parameter degeneracies and enhance cosmological inference.

Method: The authors perform a joint analysis of second- and third-order cosmic shear using the final KiDS-Legacy data release. They combine COSEBIs (Complete Orthogonal Sets of E-/B-mode Integrals) over angular scales 2'–300' with third-order aperture mass moments over 4'–32'. Key methodological improvements include: (1) an intrinsic alignment model incorporating redshift and mass dependence; (2) a baryon correction model validated against multiple hydrodynamical simulations; and (3) corrections for reduced shear and source clustering. This framework enables a consistent and robust joint inference of cosmological parameters from both Gaussian and non-Gaussian information.

作者利用KiDS-Legacy最终数据发布，对二阶与三阶宇宙剪切进行联合分析。他们将角尺度2'–300'范围内的COSEBIs（Complete Orthogonal Sets of E-/B-mode Integrals）与4'–32'范围内的三阶aperture mass矩相结合。关键方法改进包括：(1) 引入依赖红移和质量的内禀取向（intrinsic alignment）模型；(2) 使用经多个流体动力学模拟验证的重子修正模型；(3) 对减缩剪切（reduced shear）和源星系聚类（source clustering）进行修正。该框架实现了从高斯与非高斯信息中一致且稳健地联合推断宇宙学参数。

Result: The joint COSEBIs + third-order aperture mass analysis yields $Ω_m = 0.297^{+0.056}_{-0.040}$ and $S_8 = 0.806^{+0.025}_{-0.023}$. This represents a significant tightening of the $Ω_m$ constraint and more than doubles the figure of merit in the $Ω_m$--$S_8$ plane compared to a two-point-only analysis. Conclusion: Third-order cosmic shear statistics are now a mature and reliable cosmological probe that complements two-point functions by breaking key parameter degeneracies—particularly between $Ω_m$ and $σ_8$—and significantly enhancing constraining power. The consistency of these results with Planck supports the standard ΛCDM model and demonstrates that upcoming large-scale structure surveys can leverage higher-order statistics to achieve precision cosmology without systematic tensions.

Abstract: Weak lensing by large-scale structure is a powerful cosmological probe. While most analyses rely on second-order correlations, these are primarily sensitive to the parameter combination $S_8 = σ_8 (Ω_m/0.3)^{0.5}$, limiting their ability to constrain $Ω_m$ and other cosmological parameters independently. Higher-order statistics capture non-Gaussian features of the density field and can therefore break parameter degeneracies and extract more cosmological information from weak lensing surveys. We present a joint analysis of second- and third-order cosmic shear in the final data release of the Kilo-Degree Survey (KiDS-Legacy). We combine COSEBIs (Complete Orthogonal Sets of E-/B-mode Integrals) at scales between 2' and 300' with third-order aperture mass moments at scales between 4' and 32' to perform a joint analysis of second- and third-order statistics. Compared to previous KiDS analyses, we implement several methodological advances: an intrinsic alignment model with redshift and mass dependence, a baryon correction model validated on multiple hydrodynamical simulations, and corrections for reduced shear and source clustering. Combining COSEBIs with third-order aperture mass statistics in KiDS-Legacy yields $Ω_m = 0.297^{+0.056}_{-0.040}$ and $S_8 = 0.806^{+0.025}_{-0.023}$, significantly tightening the $Ω_m$ constraints and more than doubling the figure of merit in the $Ω_m$--$S_8$ plane compared to the two-point analysis alone. The third-order measurements pass stringent internal consistency tests, are fully compatible with the KiDS-Legacy 2-point constraints, other 2+3-point lensing results and with Planck CMB measurements within $1σ$, providing no evidence for an $S_8$ tension and demonstrating the maturity of 3-point cosmic shear as a key probe for forthcoming surveys.

### [57] [Bounding the Effect of HOD Assumptions on Small-Scale Clustering Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12405)
*Nick Magnelli,Zachery Brown,Lado Samushia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Small-scale galaxy clustering can strongly constrain cosmology, but only if the galaxy–halo connection (e.g., HOD parameters) is well known; without tight priors on HOD, constraints weaken dramatically—only 25% of tested cosmologies are excluded at $3σ$ in a conservative “floor” scenario vs. ~81% in an optimistic “ceiling” scenario where HOD is perfectly known.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to assess how much cosmological information from small-scale ($5$–$80$ Mpc/$h$) galaxy clustering is truly independent of assumptions about the galaxy–halo connection. Despite expectations that small scales harbor rich cosmological signal, it remains unclear whether this signal can meaningfully constrain halo-based cosmologies like $Λ$CDM without strong priors on the Halo Occupation Distribution (HOD).
\n\n
本文旨在评估来自小尺度（$5$–$80$ Mpc/$h$）星系成团性的宇宙学信息在多大程度上真正独立于星系–晕关联的假设。尽管人们预期小尺度包含丰富的宇宙学信号，但尚不清楚在缺乏对Halo Occupation Distribution (HOD) 的强先验条件下，该信号能否有效约束基于晕的宇宙学模型（如$Λ$CDM）。

Method: The authors use LRG-like mock galaxy catalogs from 81 distinct cosmologies in the {\tt \textsc{AbacusSummit}} suite. They analyze redshift-space two-point correlation function multipoles and compare two extreme HOD treatments: (1) a “floor” case using broad HOD priors and marginalizing over HOD parameters via {\tt HODmin}, a custom two-stage global optimization algorithm minimizing $χ^2$ in HOD space; and (2) a “ceiling” case assuming exact knowledge of HOD parameters. They test robustness against scale cuts, multipole choices, line-of-sight angles, simulation phases, and HOD model variants.
\n\n
作者使用{\tt \textsc{AbacusSummit}}套件中81种不同宇宙学模型生成的类LRG（Luminous Red Galaxy）mock星系目录。他们分析红移空间两点相关函数多极矩，并比较两种极端HOD处理方式：(1) “floor”情形，采用宽泛的HOD先验，并通过自研的两阶段全局优化算法{\tt HODmin}在HOD参数空间中最小化$χ^2$以边缘化HOD参数；(2) “ceiling”情形，假设HOD参数完全已知。他们还检验了尺度截断、多极矩选择、视线角度、模拟相位和HOD模型变体等因素对结果的稳健性。

Result: When comparing all 81 cosmologies to a Planck $Λ$CDM mock data vector under identical modeling assumptions, the floor analysis excludes only $25\%$ of cosmologies at $3σ$, while the ceiling excludes $\sim81\%$. Many cosmologies yield acceptable $χ^2$ in the floor case but are ruled out by orders of magnitude in $χ^2$ under the ceiling. The large gap between floor and ceiling persists across various analysis choices, indicating that small-scale clustering constraints are highly sensitive to assumed HOD knowledge.
\n\n
在相同建模假设下，将全部81种宇宙学模型与Planck $Λ$CDM mock数据向量比较时，“floor”分析仅在$3σ$排除25%的宇宙学模型，而“ceiling”排除约81%。许多宇宙学模型在“floor”情形下$χ^2$可接受，但在“ceiling”情形下被$χ^2$以多个数量级排除。“floor”与“ceiling”之间的巨大差距在各种分析选择下均存在，表明小尺度成团性约束对所假设的HOD信息高度敏感。

Conclusion: The constraining power of small-scale galaxy clustering on cosmology is not intrinsic—it critically depends on the precision of prior knowledge about the galaxy–halo connection. Without informative priors on HOD parameters, the cosmological information content of small scales is severely limited. Thus, future analyses aiming to exploit small-scale clustering must incorporate strong, physically motivated galaxy–halo priors or complementary observables to break degeneracies.
\n\n
小尺度星系成团性对宇宙学的约束能力并非内禀的——它极度依赖于对星系–晕关联先验知识的精度。若缺乏对HOD参数的信息性先验，小尺度所含的宇宙学信息将严重受限。因此，未来旨在利用小尺度成团性的分析必须引入强且物理动机明确的星系–晕先验，或结合互补观测量以打破简并。

Abstract: Small-scale galaxy clustering is expected to contain substantial cosmological information, but the extent to which this information constrains halo-based cosmologies independent of an assumed galaxy--halo connection remains unclear. We quantify this dependence using LRG-like mock galaxy catalogs built from 81 cosmologies in the {\tt \textsc{AbacusSummit}} suite. We analyze two-point correlation function multipoles on scales ranging from $5$--$80$ Mpc/$h$ and compare two limiting treatments, the \enquote{floor} and \enquote{ceiling}, of the standard five-parameter HOD model. In the conservative floor case, we impose only broad initial HOD bounds and profile over HOD parameters to determine the minimum constraining power available; we accomplish this with {\tt HODmin}, a two-stage global optimization algorithm written for minimizing $χ^2$ in HOD space. In the optimistic ceiling case, we assume the HOD parameters are known exactly. We find a significant difference between the floor and ceiling when comparing against the same Planck $Λ$CDM mock data vector with identical modeling assumptions: for the floor, $25\%$ of the discrete {\tt \textsc{AbacusSummit}} cosmologies tested are excluded at $3σ$, whereas for the ceiling, $\sim81\%$ are excluded. Many cosmologies agree well with data in the floor, and yet in the ceiling are excluded by multiple orders of magnitude in $χ^2$. We therefore observe the strength of small-scale clustering constraints depends heavily on the amount of prior HOD information assumed. We compare the sensitivity of this effect to various choices like scale cut, angle cut, multipole inclusion, mock phase, and mock HOD model. Our wide floor--ceiling bracket indicates that informative galaxy--halo priors are necessary for extracting strong small-scale clustering constraints.

</details>


### [58] [Fuzzy dark matter soliton core hosting a supermassive black hole as a dense low-mass perturber in strong gravitational lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15718)

The boosted central density due to the SMBH reduces tidal stripping and increases lensing impact, matching observations of a $\\sim 10^6 M_\\odot$ object inconsistent with standard astrophysical bodies. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Observations of strong lens systems have uncovered compact, dense substructures with masses around $10^6 M_\\odot$ that do not correspond to any known stellar or dark matter structures in standard cosmological models (e.g., CDM predicts numerous low-mass halos, but their density profiles are typically too diffuse to cause the observed lensing anomalies). The fuzzy dark matter model naturally produces solitonic cores at the centers of halos, but these alone may be insufficiently dense. The presence of a central SMBH can significantly steepen the soliton's density profile, making it a viable candidate for explaining these puzzling lensing perturbers.
\n\n
强引力透镜系统的观测揭示了质量约$10^6 M_\\odot$的致密子结构，这些结构无法用标准宇宙学模型（如冷暗物质CDM）中的已知恒星或暗物质结构解释（CDM虽预言大量低质量晕，但其密度轮廓通常过于弥散，难以产生观测到的透镜异常）。模糊暗物质（FDM）模型自然地在晕中心形成孤子核，但其自身密度可能仍不足。中心超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的存在可显著陡化孤子密度轮廓，使其成为解释此类透镜扰动体的有力候选。

Method: The authors model a soliton core in the FDM framework modified by the gravitational potential of a central SMBH. They solve the Schr\\"odinger-Poisson equations including the SMBH contribution to obtain the enhanced soliton density profile. This profile is then compared with the inferred mass distribution of the perturber in the strong lens system JVAS B1938+666. They also discuss astrophysical formation channels for the required $\\sim 10^5 M_\\odot$ SMBH, such as direct collapse black holes or primordial black holes, to assess plausibility.
\n\n
作者在模糊暗物质（FDM）框架下构建包含中心超大质量黑洞（SMBH）引力势修正的孤子核模型。通过求解包含SMBH贡献的Schr\\"odinger-Poisson方程，获得增强后的孤子密度轮廓，并将其与强透镜系统JVAS B1938+666中推断出的扰动体质量分布进行对比。同时讨论了所需$\\sim 10^5 M_\\odot$量级SMBH的天体物理形成通道（如直接坍缩黑洞或原初黑洞），以评估该模型的合理性。

Result: The SMBH-boosted soliton in the FDM model with particle mass $m = 4 \\times 10^{-21}$ eV and SMBH mass $M_{\\rm BH} = 4 \\times 10^5 M_\\odot$ reproduces the observed mass profile of the $\\sim 10^6 M_\\odot$ perturber in JVAS B1938+666. The enhanced central density makes the soliton more resilient to tidal disruption and increases its gravitational lensing signal, resolving the tension with standard astrophysical interpretations.

Conclusion: The SMBH-enhanced soliton core in the FDM paradigm provides a compelling explanation for anomalous dense perturbers in strong lensing without invoking exotic baryonic objects or fine-tuned CDM subhalos. This supports FDM as a viable alternative to CDM on small scales and links dark matter microphysics to SMBH formation scenarios.
\n\n
FDM范式中由SMBH增强的孤子核为强引力透镜中的异常致密扰动体提供了一种令人信服的解释，无需引入奇异的重子天体或精细调节的CDM子晕。这支持FDM作为小尺度上CDM的可行替代理论，并将暗物质微观物理与超大质量黑洞形成机制联系起来。

Abstract: Recent high-resolution imaging observations of strong lens systems reveal dense low-mass perturbers. We propose a soliton core, whose central density is boosted by a supermassive black hole (SMBH), in the fuzzy dark matter (FDM) model as an efficient perturber in strong gravitational lensing. The higher central density makes it less efficient in the tidal mass loss, and leads to the higher impact in gravitational lensing. We show that the mass profile of a $\sim 10^6M_\odot$ perturber in JVAS B1938+666, which does not resemble any known astronomical object, can be well explained by a soliton core in the FDM model with the mass of $4\times 10^{-21}$eV hosting an SMBH with the mass of $4\times 10^5M_\odot$. The high mass of the SMBH may be explained by several scenarios that predict heavy SMBH seeds such as the direct collapse black hole formation and primordial black holes.

</details>
